	getFishingCastPosition = function()
		local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoidRootPart then
			return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		end
		return humanoidRootPart.Position + humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * 35
	end

	equipFishingRod = function()
		if not game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character or not game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack then
			return
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") and string.find(child.Name, "Rod") then
				game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(child)
				return
			end
		end
	end

		end
	end

		findNearbyLocation = function(arg)
			local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
			character = character and character.PrimaryPart

			for _, child in pairs(locations2:GetChildren()) do
				if child and child.Name == arg and character and (child.Position - character.Position).Magnitude < 1500 then

				if child:IsA("Tool") then
					if arg ~= "Melee" or child.ToolTip ~= "Melee" and child.Name ~= "Combat" then
						if arg ~= "Sword" or child.ToolTip ~= "Sword" then
							if arg == "Gun" and child.ToolTip == "Gun" then
								return child.Name
							end

							if arg == "Fruit" and child.ToolTip == "Blox Fruit" then
								return child.Name
							end
							continue
						end

						return child.Name
					end

					return child.Name
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		end

						owner = tostring(owner.Value) == name
					end

					if owner then
						if v_82 then
							return child
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		GetAllBladeHits = function()
			bladehits = {}

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				if child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (child.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= CONST[79] then
					table.insert(bladehits, child)
				end
			end

			return bladehits
		end

		Getplayerhit = function()
			bladehits = {}

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (child.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= CONST[79] then
					table.insert(bladehits, child)
				end
			end

			return bladehits
		end

		get_Monster = function()
			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				local upperTorso = child:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or child:FindFirstChild("Head")

				if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) and upperTorso then
					if (child.Head.Position - localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 50 then
						return true, upperTorso.Position
					end
				end
			end

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
				if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
					return true, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position
				end
			end

			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
				if child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 and child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
					return true, child.Engine.Position
				end
			end
		end

					end

					RegisterHit:FireServer(unpack(tbl27))
				end
			end
		end

			local n34 = n32 - math.floor(arg / 60 / 60) * v_83

			if n34 < 10 then
				n34 = "0" .. tostring(n34) or n34

	handlers = { Managers = {} }

	local lib3 = loadstring([[

    local module = {}
    module.__index = module

    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

    local tweens = {}
    local EasingStyle = Enum.EasingStyle.Linear

    function module.new(obj, time, prop, value)
      local self = setmetatable({}, module)

      self.tween = TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(time, EasingStyle), { [prop] = value })
      self.tween:Play()
      self.value = value
      self.object = obj

      if tweens[obj] then
        tweens[obj]:destroy()
      end

      tweens[obj] = self
      return self
    end

    function module:destroy()
      self.tween:Pause()
      self.tween:Destroy()

      tweens[self.object] = nil
      setmetatable(self, nil)
    end

    function module:stop(obj)
      if tweens[obj] then
        tweens[obj]:destroy()
      end
    end

    return module
  ]])()

			local v_87 = nil

			while true do
				local v_88

					break

						huge = magnitude
						v_86 = v_85
						v_87 = v_88
					end
				end
			end

			return v_87, v_86
		end

				if arg.NpcDebounce and arg2[arg.nextNum] then
					new(arg2[arg.nextNum] + arg.SpawnVector)
					return nil
				end
				local primaryPart = localPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart

				if #arg2 ~= 1 then
					if #arg2 > 1 then
						if #arg2 < arg.nextNum then
							arg.nextNum = 1
						end

						local v_83 = arg2[arg.nextNum]

						if primaryPart and (primaryPart.Position - v_83.Position).Magnitude < 5 then
							arg.nextNum = arg.nextNum + 1
							arg.NpcDebounce = true
							task.delay(1, fn34)
						else
							arg.new(v_83, arg3)
						end
					end
				else
					arg.new(arg2[1], arg3)
				end
			end
		end

		Distance = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local vector = Vector3.new(arg.X, not arg3 and arg.Y or 0, arg.Z)

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				if not arg2 then
					local character

					while true do
						character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and localplayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not (character and character.Position) then
							wait(0.5)
							continue
						end
						break
					end

					arg2 = character.Position
				end

				return (vector - Vector3.new(arg2.X, not arg3 and arg2.Y or 0, arg2.Z)).magnitude
			end)

			if ok then
				return result
			end
			warn("Distance Calculation Failed", result)
			return nil
		end

		Check_Inventory = function(...)
			for _, v_83 in pairs({ ... }) do
				if localPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(v_83) or localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(v_83) then
					return true
				end
			end

			return false
		end

		Is_raiding = function()
			return localPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible or StartingRaid
		end

		Check_raid_chip = function()
			local function fn35(arg)
				for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name:lower():find("microchip") then
						return true
					end
				end

				return false
			end

			return fn35(localPlayer.Backpack) or fn35(localPlayer.Character)
		end

		Check_fruit_inventory = function()
			local function fn35(arg)
				for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name:lower():find("fruit") then
						return true
					end
				end

				return false
			end

			return fn35(localPlayer.Backpack) or fn35(localPlayer.Character)
		end

		local function fn35(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local v_83 = Distance(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, arg2.Position, true)
			local v_84 = v_83
			local n31 = 0
			lib3.new(arg, arg3, "CFrame", arg2)

			task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					if arg and arg.Parent and n31 ~= -1 then
						task.wait(0.1)
						v_84 = Distance(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, arg2.Position, true)

						if v_83 + 30 < v_84 then
							n31 = -1
							lib3:stop(arg)
							break
						else
							v_83 = v_84
							continue
						end
					end

					break
				end
			end)
		end

		tbl27.new = function(lastCF_, arg, arg2, arg3)
			local v_83 = tbl27
			local flag14 = isAlive(localPlayer.Character)

			if flag14 then
				local lastTP = v_83.lastTP
				flag14 = tick() - lastTP >= 1 or lastCF_ ~= v_83.lastCF
			end

			if flag14 then
				if localPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart then
					if not arg2 then
						v_83.lastPosition = lastCF_.Position
					end

					v_83.lastTP = tick()
					v_83.lastCF = lastCF_
					local humanoid = localPlayer.Character.Humanoid
					local primaryPart = localPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart

					if humanoid.Sit then
						humanoid.Sit = false
					elseif primaryPart.Anchored then
						lib3:stop(primaryPart)
					else
						local tweenSpeed = rzSettings.TweenSpeed
						local position = lastCF_.Position
						local magnitude = (primaryPart.Position - position).Magnitude

						if magnitude < 30 and not arg then
							lib3:stop(primaryPart)
							primaryPart.CFrame = lastCF_
						end

						if sea == 3 or sea == 2 or sea == 1 then
							local nearestPortal, v_84 = tbl27:GetNearestPortal(position)
							local n31

							if nearestPortal then
								if sea == 3 then
									n31 = (position - nearestPortal).Magnitude + 8000
								else
									n31 = (position - nearestPortal).Magnitude + 10000
								end
							else
								n31 = nearestPortal
							end

							if nearestPortal then
								local bypassCooldown = v_83.BypassCooldown
								nearestPortal = tick() - bypassCooldown >= 8 and n31 < magnitude
							end

							if nearestPortal then
								if sea == 3 then
									local vector = Vector3.new(11539, -2000, 9831)
									local magnitude2 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector).Magnitude
									local vector2 = Vector3.new(28286.35546875, 14897, 103)
									local magnitude3 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector2).Magnitude
									local vector3 = Vector3.new(-12462, 375, -7552)
									local magnitude4 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector3).Magnitude
									local vector4 = Vector3.new(5651, 1015, -350)
									local magnitude5 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector4).Magnitude
									local vector5 = Vector3.new(-5119, 315, -2964)
									local magnitude6 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector5).Magnitude
									local vector6 = Vector3.new(-16456, 527, 436)
									local magnitude7 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector6).Magnitude

									if magnitude2 <= 8000 then
										tbl27:TeleportToNormal()
									elseif v_84 == "Submerged Island" then
										tbl27:TeleportToSub()
									elseif v_84 == "Tiki Outpost" then
										tbl27:TeleportToTiki()
									elseif v_84 == "Temple of Time" then
										tbl27:TeleportToTemple()
									elseif v_84 == "Mansion" then
										tbl27:TeleportToMansion()
									elseif v_84 == "Sea Castle" and magnitude4 <= 6000 then
										tbl27:TeleportToCastleMan()
									elseif v_84 == "Sea Castle" and magnitude5 <= 6000 then
										tbl27:TeleportToCastleHydra()
									elseif v_84 == "Sea Castle" and unlocked["Feathered Visage"] and unlocked["Valkyrie Helm"] and magnitude7 <= 6000 then
										tbl27:TeleportToCastleTiki()
									elseif v_84 == "Hydra Island" and magnitude6 <= 6000 then
										tbl27:TeleportToCastlesHydra()
									elseif not CheckIfHas() and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Compass.Frame.DangerLevel.Visible then
										local v_85 = 0
										game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health = v_85
									elseif magnitude3 <= 8000 then
										game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
									elseif magnitude > 8000 and game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Compass.Frame.DangerLevel.Visible then
										game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
									else
										fn35(primaryPart, lastCF_, magnitude / tweenSpeed)
									end
								elseif sea == 2 then
									local vector = Vector3.new(926, 125, 32851)
									local magnitude2 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector).Magnitude

									if v_84 == "Cursed Ship" then
										tbl27:TeleportToCursedShip()
									elseif v_84 == "Flamingo Room" then
										tbl27:TeleportToRoom()
									elseif v_84 == "Flamingo Mansion" then
										tbl27:TeleportToFlamingo()
									elseif magnitude2 <= 6000 then
										tbl27:TeleportToZombie()
									else
										fn35(primaryPart, lastCF_, magnitude / tweenSpeed)
									end
								elseif sea == 1 then
									local vector = Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 11.680007934570312, 1819.7840576171875)
									local magnitude2 = (game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - vector).Magnitude

									if v_84 == "Under Water Island" then
										tbl27:TeleportToUnderWater()
									elseif v_84 == "Sky Island 2" then
										tbl27:TeleportToSkyland2()
									elseif v_84 == "Sky Island 1" then
										tbl27:TeleportToSkyland1()
									elseif magnitude2 <= 6000 then
										tbl27:TeleportToEntrance()
									else
										fn35(primaryPart, lastCF_, magnitude / tweenSpeed)
									end
								end
							elseif arg then
								fn35(primaryPart, lastCF_, magnitude / arg)
							else
								fn35(primaryPart, lastCF_, magnitude / tweenSpeed)
							end
						else
							if not arg3 then
								local position2 = primaryPart.Position
								local cframe = CFrame.new(position2.X, position.Y, position2.Z)

								if (position2 - cframe.Position).Magnitude > 75 then
									lib3:stop(primaryPart)
									task.wait(0.1)
									primaryPart.CFrame = cframe
									task.wait(0.5)
								end
							end

							if magnitude < 380 then
								fn35(primaryPart, lastCF_, magnitude / tweenSpeed * 2)
							else
								fn35(primaryPart, lastCF_, magnitude / tweenSpeed)
							end
						end
					end

					return
				end

				return nil
			end

			return nil
		end

		lib2.Tween:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
			if not lib2.Tween.Parent and isAlive(localPlayer.Character) then
				lib3:stop(localPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart)
			end
		end)

		new = tbl27.new
		return tbl27
	end

	managers.QuestManager = function()
		local tbl27 = {
			QuestList = {},
			EnemyList = {},
			QuestPos = {},
			Crafts = {},
			Sea = lib2.GameData.Sea,
			takeQuestDebounce = false,
			_Position = CFrame.new(0, 0, 2.5),
		}

		local tbl28 = { GuideModule = ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("GuideModule"), Quests = ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("Quests") }

		local function fn34(arg)
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return require(tbl28[arg])
			end)

			if not ok then
				warn(("falha a o carregar Module [ %s ] [ %s ]"):format(arg, result))
			end

			return ok and result and result or loadstring(httpGet(workspace, "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Nguoidungcode/Roblox-/refs/heads/main/GameModules/" .. arg .. ".lua"))()
		end

		local GuideModule = fn34("GuideModule")
		local Quests = fn34("Quests")
		local enemyLocations = lib2.EnemyLocations
		local isBoss = lib2.IsBoss

		local function fn35(arg)
			local v_83 = next
			local task_ = arg.Task
			local tbl29 = {}
			local tbl30 = {}
			local v_84 = nil

			while true do
				local v_85 = v_83(task_, v_84)

				if v_85 == nil then
					break
				else
					tbl30 = enemyLocations[v_85] or {}
					enemyLocations[v_85] = tbl30
					table.insert(tbl29, v_85)
					v_84 = v_85
				end
			end

			return tbl29, tbl30
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			if GuideModule.Data.IsFakeData then
				return nil
			end
			local v_83, v_84, v_85 = pairs(GuideModule.Data.NPCList)

			while true do
				local v_86, v_87 = v_83(v_84, v_85)

				if v_86 ~= nil then
					tbl27.QuestPos[v_87.NPCName] = CFrame.new(v_87.Position)
					v_85 = v_86
					continue
				end

				break
			end

			setmetatable(GuideModule.Data.NPCList, { __newindex = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
				tbl27.QuestPos[arg3.NPCName] = CFrame.new(arg3.Position)
				return rawset(arg, arg2, arg3)
			end })
		end)

		task.spawn(lib2.RunFunctions.Quests, tbl27, Quests, fn35)

		tbl27.GetQuest = function(arg)
			if arg.oldLevel ~= level.Value or not arg.CurrentQuest then
				arg.oldLevel = level.Value
				local sea = arg.Sea
				local n31 = math.clamp(level.Value, 0, sea == 1 and 700 or sea == 2 and 1500 or level.Value)
				local v_83, v_84, v_85 = ipairs(arg.QuestList)
				local v_86 = nil

				while true do
					local v_87, v_88 = v_83(v_84, v_85)

					if v_87 == nil then
						arg.CurrentQuest = v_86
						return
					else
						local level = v_88.Enemy.Level
						local v_89 = v_88.Enemy.Name[1]
						if isBoss(v_89) or v_89 == "Wysper" or v_89 == CONST[85] or v_89 == "Magma Admiral" then
							v_85 = v_87
							continue
						end

						if not (n31 < level) then
							v_85 = v_87
							v_86 = v_88
							continue
						end

						break
					end
				end

				arg.CurrentQuest = v_86
				return v_86
			end

			return arg.CurrentQuest
		end

		tbl27.GetQuestPosition = function(arg, arg2)
			if not GuideModule.Data.IsFakeData then
				return arg.QuestPos[GuideModule.Data.LastClosestNPC]
			end
			local v_83 = GuideModule.Data.NPCs[arg2]

			if v_83 then
				v_83 = npCs:FindFirstChild(v_83) or ReplicatedStorage2.NPCs:FindFirstChild(v_83)
			end

			return v_83 and v_83:GetPivot()
		end

		tbl27.VerifyQuest = function(arg, arg2)
			local trackedQuestFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TrackedQuestFrame")
			if not trackedQuestFrame then
				return false
			end

			local function fn36(arg3)
				return (tostring(arg3):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("-", ""))
			end

			local v_83 = fn36(trackedQuestFrame.Frame.header.textLabel.Text)
			if type(arg2) == "string" then
				local v_84 = 1
				return string.find(v_83, fn36(arg2), v_84, true) ~= nil
			end

			for _, v_84 in ipairs(arg2) do
				local v_85 = 1
				if string.find(v_83, fn36(v_84), v_85, true) then
					return v_84
				end
			end
		end

		tbl27.StartQuest = function(arg, teleportingToNpc, arg2, arg3)
			if arg3 and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(arg3.Position) >= 5 then
				new(arg3 * arg._Position)
				return "Teleporting to NPC: " .. teleportingToNpc
			end

			if not arg.takeQuestDebounce then
				task.wait(0.5)
				lib2.FireRemote("StartQuest", teleportingToNpc, arg2)
				return "Getting Quest: " .. teleportingToNpc, task.wait(0.5)
			end

			local debounce = arg.Debounce
			local flag14

			if debounce then
				local debounce2 = arg.Debounce
				flag14 = tick() - debounce2 < 75 and arg.InDebounceQuest == arg2 .. teleportingToNpc
			else
				flag14 = debounce
			end

			if flag14 then
				local debounce2 = arg.Debounce
				return "Quest Debounce: " .. tbl26.GetTimer(75 - tick() - debounce2)
			end
			rzSettings.RunningMethod = "Getting Quest: " .. teleportingToNpc
			task.wait(0.5)
			lib2.FireRemote("StartQuest", teleportingToNpc, arg2)
			arg.Debounce = tick()
			arg.InDebounceQuest = arg2 .. teleportingToNpc
			return rzSettings.RunningMethod, task.wait(0.5)
		end

		return tbl27
	end

	managers.FarmManager = function()

						if tick() - now2 >= 1 then
							equipTool()
						end

						EnableBuso()
						local farmDistance = rzSettings.FarmDistance
						n31 += 3.5 * v_83
						local v_84 = 8
						new(CFrame.new(math.cos(n31) * farmDistance, v_84, math.sin(n31) * farmDistance) + arg2.Position)
						v_83 = task.wait(rzSettings.SmoothMode and 0.1 or 0)
					end
				end,

					local n31 = arg2.CFrame + rzSettings.FarmPos

					if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(n31.Position) >= 5 then
						new(n31)
					end
				end,
			},

		tbl27.ToolDebounce = function()
			now2 = tick()
		end

		tbl27.TargetPosition = function(arg)
			if typeof(arg) == "CFrame" then
				new(arg)
				EnableBuso()
				equipTool()
			end
		end

		tbl27.GetNextAxis = function(arg)
			local axisDebounce = arg.axisDebounce
			if tick() - axisDebounce <= 0.4 then
				return arg.nextAxis
			end
			local v_85 = Vector3[math.random() <= 0.5 and "xAxis" or "zAxis"]
			local v_86 = 0.5
			local nextAxis = v_85 * (math.random() <= v_86 and rzSettings.FarmDistance or -rzSettings.FarmDistance) + Vector3.yAxis * 8
			local now3 = tick()
			arg.nextAxis = nextAxis
			arg.axisDebounce = now3
			return nextAxis
		end

		tbl27.Mastery = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			if not arg2 or not arg3 then
				return
			end

			if not arg2.Parent then
				return
			end

			if not arg3.MaxHealth or arg3.MaxHealth == 0 then
				return
			end

			if not rzSettings or not rzSettings.FarmPos then
				return
			end
			local n33 = arg2.CFrame + rzSettings.FarmPos
			local n34 = arg3.Health / arg3.MaxHealth * 100
			equipTool(n34 <= rzSettings.mHealth and rzSettings.mTool or "Melee", true)

			if lib2 and lib2.BringEnemies then
				lib2:BringEnemies(arg2.Parent)
			end

			EnableBuso()

			if localPlayer and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(n33.Position) >= 2.5 then
				new(n33)
			end

			local flag14 = n34 <= rzSettings.mHealth

			if flag14 then
				local v_85 = 5
				flag14 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(n33.Position) < v_85
			end

			if flag14 then
				UseSkills(arg2, _G.SelectMasterySkill)
			end
		end

		tbl27.attack = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
			local v_85 = arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			if not v_85 then
				return nil
			end

			if rzEnabledOptions.Mastery and v_85.MaxHealth < CONST[162] then
				tbl27:Mastery(arg.PrimaryPart, v_85)
				return true
			end

			if arg2 then
				lib2:BringEnemies(arg, arg3)
			end

			if tick() - now2 >= 1 then
				equipTool()
			end

			EnableBuso()
			tbl27.FarmModes[arg4 or rzSettings.FarmMode](tbl27, arg.PrimaryPart, arg4)
			local smoothMode = rzSettings.SmoothMode

			if smoothMode then
				smoothMode = (arg4 or rzSettings.FarmMode) ~= "Orbit" and task.wait(0.1) and isAlive(arg) and arg.PrimaryPart and rzEnabledOptions[rzSettings.RunningOption]
			end

			if smoothMode then
				local v_86 = rzSettings
				local runningMethod = "Killing: " .. arg.Name
				env.OnFarm = true
				v_86.RunningMethod = runningMethod
				tbl27.attack(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
			end

			return true
		end

		tbl27.Material = function(arg, farmingMaterial)
			local v_85 = tbl28[farmingMaterial] and tbl28[farmingMaterial][lib2.GameData.Sea]
			local v_86 = tbl30[farmingMaterial]

			if v_86 then
				local enemyLocation = arg.EnemyLocation
				local canFarm = arg.CanFarm

				if canFarm[farmingMaterial] == nil then
					if v_86[2][lib2.GameData.Sea] then
						canFarm[farmingMaterial] = true
					else
						canFarm[farmingMaterial] = false
					end
				end

				if not canFarm[farmingMaterial] then
					return nil
				end

				if enemyLocation[farmingMaterial] == nil then
					local v_87, v_88, v_89 = ipairs(v_86[1])

					while true do
						local v_90, v_91 = v_87(v_88, v_89)

						if v_90 == nil then
							break
						else
							local v_92 = enemyLocations[v_91]

							if v_92 and #v_92 > 0 then
								enemyLocation[farmingMaterial] = v_92
								v_89 = v_90
							else
								v_89 = v_90
							end
						end
					end

					if not enemyLocation[farmingMaterial] then
						enemyLocation[farmingMaterial] = false
					end
				end

				local v_87, v_88, v_89 = ipairs(v_86[1])
				local killing, v_90

				while true do
					local v_91
					v_91, killing = v_87(v_88, v_89)

					if v_91 == nil then
						if enemyLocation[farmingMaterial] then
							managers.PlayerTeleport:NPCs(enemyLocation[farmingMaterial])
						else
							new(v_85)
						end

						return "Farming Material: " .. farmingMaterial
					else
						v_90 = enemySpawned(killing)
						if v_90 and v_90.PrimaryPart then
							break
						end
						v_89 = v_91
					end
				end

				arg.attack(v_90, true, true)
				return "Killing: " .. killing
			end
		end

		tbl27.GetNpcPosition = function(arg, arg2)
			if arg.NPCs[arg2] then
				return arg.NPCs[arg2]:GetPivot()
			end
			local v_85 = npCs:FindFirstChild(arg2) or ReplicatedStorage2.NPCs:FindFirstChild(arg2)
			if v_85 then
				arg.NPCs[arg2] = v_85
				return v_85:GetPivot()
			end
		end

		return tbl27
	end

	managers.RaidManager = function()
		if lib2.GameData.Sea ~= 2 and lib2.GameData.Sea ~= 3 then
			return nil
		end

		return {
			RaidPosition = CFrame.new(-5036, 315, -2950),
			requests = {},
			Require = 0,
			Timer = localPlayer.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("Main").Timer,
			Button = lib2.GameData.Sea == 2 and "CircleIsland.RaidSummon2.Button.Main" or lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 and "Boat Castle.RaidSummon2.Button.Main" or false,
			IsRaiding = function()
				return rzEnabledOptions.Raid and localPlayer:GetAttribute("IslandRaiding")
			end,
			GetRaidIsland = function()
				return lib2:GetRaidIsland()
			end,
			CanStartRaid = function()
				local flag14

				if level.Value < 1200 then
					flag14 = false
				else
					flag14 = VerifyTool("Special Microchip")
				end

				return flag14
			end,
			start = function(arg)
				if not arg:IsRaiding() and arg:CanStartRaid() then
					local parts = arg.Button:split(".")
					local v_83 = map

					for i = 1, #parts do
						v_83 = v_83 and v_83:FindFirstChild(parts[i])
					end

					if v_83 and v_83:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector") then
						fireclickdetector(v_83.ClickDetector)
						task.wait(1)
					elseif lib2.GameData.Sea == 2 then
						new(CFrame.new(-6542, 313, -4735))
					elseif lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 then
						new(CFrame.new(-5119, 315, -2964))
					end
				end
			end,
			requestFragment = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
				if arg.requests[arg2] then
					return nil
				end
				arg.Require = arg.Require + (arg3 or 0)
			end,
		}
	end

	managers.ItemsQuests = function()
		local tbl27 = { CursedDualKatana = {}, SkullGuitar = {} }
		local isSpawned = lib2.Enemies.IsSpawned
		local enemySpawned = lib2.EnemySpawned
		local enemyLocations = lib2.EnemyLocations
		local equipTool = lib2.EquipTool
		local fireRemote = lib2.FireRemote

		if lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 then
			local v_83 = nil

			local function fn34()
				if v_83 and v_83.Value > 0 then
					return v_83
				end
				local huge = math.huge
				local v_84, v_85, v_86 = ipairs(localPlayer.QuestHaze:GetChildren())
				local v_87 = nil

				while true do
					local v_88, v_89 = v_84(v_85, v_86)

					if v_88 == nil then
						break
					elseif v_89.Value > 0 then
						local attribute = v_89:GetAttribute("Position")
						local v_90

						if typeof(attribute) ~= "Vector3" then
							v_90 = false
						else
							v_90 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(attribute)
						end

						if v_90 then
							if v_90 <= huge then
								huge = v_90
								v_87 = v_89
								v_86 = v_88
							else
								v_86 = v_88
							end
						else
							v_86 = v_88
						end
					else
						v_86 = v_88
					end
				end

				v_83 = v_87
				return v_87
			end

			local function fn35(arg)
				for i = 1, 3 do
					local v_84 = arg:FindFirstChild("Torch" .. i)

					if v_84 then
						if v_84:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
							if v_84.ProximityPrompt.Enabled then
								return v_84
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function fn36(arg)
				for i = 1, 3 do
					local v_84 = arg:FindFirstChild("Pedestal" .. i)

					if v_84 then
						if v_84:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
							if v_84.ProximityPrompt.Enabled then
								return v_84
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function fn37(arg)
				local huge = math.huge
				local v_84, v_85, v_86 = ipairs(ReplicatedStorage2.NPCs:GetChildren())
				local v_87 = nil

				while true do
					local v_88, v_89 = v_84(v_85, v_86)

					if v_88 == nil then
						break
					elseif v_89.Name == "Boat Dealer" and not arg[v_89] then
						local primaryPart = v_89.PrimaryPart

						if primaryPart and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(primaryPart.Position) <= huge then
							huge = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(primaryPart.Position)
							v_86 = v_88
							v_87 = v_89
						else
							v_86 = v_88
						end
					else
						v_86 = v_88
					end
				end

				return v_87
			end

			tbl27.CursedDualKatana.Yama = {
				function(arg)
					if VerifyTool("Yama") then
						equipTool("Yama")
						local forestPirate = enemySpawned("Forest Pirate")

						if forestPirate and forestPirate.PrimaryPart then
							new(forestPirate.PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -2))

							for _, child in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("Tool") then
									child.Parent = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack
								end
							end
						else
							new(arg.ForestPirate)
						end
					else
						fireRemote("LoadItem", "Yama")
					end

					return true
				end,
				function()
					local questHaze = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("QuestHaze") and fn34()

					if questHaze then
						local name = questHaze.Name
						local v_84 = enemySpawned(name)

						if v_84 and v_84.PrimaryPart then
							managers.FarmManager.attack(v_84, true)
						elseif enemyLocations[name] then
							managers.PlayerTeleport:NPCs(enemyLocations[name])
						else
							new(questHaze:GetAttribute("Position"))
						end

						return true
					end
				end,
				function(arg, arg2)
					local hellDimension = map:FindFirstChild("HellDimension")

					if hellDimension then
						local v_84 = fn35(hellDimension) or hellDimension:FindFirstChild("Exit")

						if v_84 and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(v_84.Position) <= 600 then
							local v_85 = enemySpawned(arg.Hell)
							if v_85 and v_85.PrimaryPart then
								new(v_85.PrimaryPart.CFrame + rzSettings.FarmPos)
								return true, lib2.KillAura(125)
							end
							local flag14 = v_84.Name == "Exit"

							if not flag14 then
								local v_86 = 5
								flag14 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(v_84.Position) >= v_86
							end

							if flag14 then
								new(v_84.CFrame)
							else
								fireproximityprompt(v_84.ProximityPrompt)
							end
						end

						return true
					end

					if not isSpawned("Soul Reaper") then
						return arg2.SoulReaper() or arg2.Bones(), fireRemote("Bones", "Buy", 1, 1)
					end
					local soulReaper = enemySpawned("Soul Reaper")

					if soulReaper and soulReaper.PrimaryPart and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(soulReaper.PrimaryPart.Position) > 6 then
						new(soulReaper.PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -2))

						for _, child in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Tool") then
								child.Parent = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack
							end
						end

						return true
					end
				end,
			}

			tbl27.CursedDualKatana.Tushita = {
				function(arg)
					if localPlayer:FindFirstChild("BoatQuest") then
						local currentDealer = arg.CurrentDealer

						if not currentDealer or arg.BoatsDealer[currentDealer] then
							currentDealer = npCs:FindFirstChild("Boat Dealer")

							if not currentDealer or not currentDealer.PrimaryPart or arg.BoatsDealer[currentDealer] then
								currentDealer = fn37(arg.BoatsDealer)
							end
						end

						if currentDealer and currentDealer.PrimaryPart then
							if arg.CurrentDealer ~= currentDealer then
								arg.CurrentDealer = currentDealer
							end

							if 5 <= localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(currentDealer.PrimaryPart.Position) then
								return true, new(currentDealer.PrimaryPart.CFrame)
							end

							if fireRemote("CDKQuest", "BoatQuest", currentDealer, "Check") then
								fireRemote("CDKQuest", "BoatQuest", currentDealer)
							end

							arg.BoatsDealer[currentDealer] = true
						else
							task.wait(0.5)
						end
					end
				end,
				function(arg, arg2)
					return arg2.PirateRaid()
				end,
				function(arg)
					local v_84 = map:FindFirstChild(CONST[148])

					if v_84 then
						local exit = fn35(v_84) or v_84:FindFirstChild("Exit")

						if exit and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(exit.Position) <= 600 then
							local v_85 = enemySpawned(arg.Heaven)
							if v_85 and v_85.PrimaryPart then
								new(v_85.PrimaryPart.CFrame + rzSettings.FarmPos)
								return true, lib2.KillAura(125)
							end

							if exit.Name == "Exit" or localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(exit.Position) >= 5 then
								new(exit.CFrame)
							else
								fireproximityprompt(exit.ProximityPrompt)
							end
						end

						return true
					end

					if isSpawned("Cake Queen") then
						local cakeQueen = enemySpawned("Cake Queen")

						if cakeQueen and cakeQueen.PrimaryPart then
							managers.FarmManager.attack(cakeQueen)
						else
							new(arg.CakeQueen)
						end

						return true
					end
				end,
			}

			tbl27.CursedDualKatana.FinalQuest = function(arg)
				if VerifyTool("Tushita") or VerifyTool("Yama") then
					if isSpawned("Cursed Skeleton Boss") then
						local v_84 = enemySpawned("Cursed Skeleton Boss")
						if not (v_84 and v_84.PrimaryPart) then
							return nil
						end
						equipTool("Sword", true)
						managers.FarmManager.ToolDebounce()
						managers.FarmManager.attack(v_84)
						return true
					end

					if localPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible then
						VirtualUser:ClickButton1(Vector2.new(10000, 10000))
					end

					local v_84 = fn36(map.Turtle.Cursed)

					if v_84 then
						if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(v_84.Position) >= 5 then
							new(v_84.CFrame)
						else
							fireproximityprompt(v_84.ProximityPrompt)
						end

						return true
					end

					local v_85 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(arg.CursedSkeleton[1].Position)

					if v_85 > 6 then
						new(arg.CursedSkeleton[1], v_85 <= 100 and 40 or false)
					else
						new(arg.CursedSkeleton[2])
					end

					task.wait(0.5)
					return true
				end

				fireRemote("LoadItem", "Yama")
			end
		end

		if lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 then
			local cframe = CFrame.new(5867, CONST[14], 872)
			local cframe2 = CFrame.new(5771, CONST[154], 804)
			local rfInteractDragonQuest = net:WaitForChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest")
			local count = lib2.Inventory.Count
			local unlocked = lib2.Inventory.Unlocked
			local handlers2 = { Progress = {}, CurrentBelt = "Null" }

			local tbl28 = {
				CheckStart = { "CanTransform", "CanLearnTether", "TetherLearned", "AvailableVQuest" },
				Complete = { NPC = "Dragon Wizard", Command = CONST[109], Action = "Complete" },
				Begin = { NPC = "Dragon Wizard", Command = "Ascension", Action = "Begin" },
				LearnTether = { NPC = "Dragon Wizard", Command = "LearnTether" },
				BuyDraco = { NPC = "Dragon Wizard", Command = "DragonRace" },
			}

			local tbl29 = {
				DojoClaim = { NPC = "Dojo Trainer", Command = "ClaimQuest" },
				DojoProgress = { NPC = "Dojo Trainer", Command = "RequestQuest" },
				SpeakWizard = { NPC = "Dragon Wizard", Command = "Speak" },
			}

			handlers2.CollectReward = function(arg, arg2)
				if unlocked["Dojo Belt (" .. arg2 .. ")"] then
					handlers2.Progress[arg2] = nil
					return nil
				end

				if 3 < localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(cframe.Position) then
					new(cframe)
				else
					rfInteractDragonQuest:InvokeServer(tbl29.DojoClaim)
					tbl27.CurrentBeltQuest = nil
				end

				return true
			end

			handlers2.White = function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.Progress.White < 20 then
					return arg2.Level()
				end
				return arg:CollectReward("White")
			end

			handlers2.Green = function(arg)
				if arg.Progress.Green >= 330 then
					return arg:CollectReward("Green")
				end

				if localPlayer:GetAttribute("DangerLevel") then
					if localPlayer:GetAttribute("DangerLevel") >= 500 and arg.GreenTimer then
						local greenTimer = arg.GreenTimer
						arg.Progress.Green = arg.Progress.Green + tick() - greenTimer
					end

					arg.GreenTimer = tick()
				end

				if managers.SeaManager:GetPlayerBoat() then
					managers.SeaManager:RandomTeleport("inf")
				else
					managers.SeaManager:BuyNewBoat()
				end

				return true
			end

			handlers2.Purple = function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.Progress.Purple >= 3 then
					return arg:CollectReward("Purple")
				end

				if arg.PurpleProgress then
					if arg2.EliteHunter() then
						return true
					end
					local purpleProgress = arg.PurpleProgress
					arg.Progress.Purple = arg.StartPurpleProgress + lib2:GetProgress("EliteProgress", "EliteHunter", "Progress") - purpleProgress
				else
					arg.StartPurpleProgress = arg.Progress.Purple
					arg.PurpleProgress = lib2:GetProgress("EliteProgress", "EliteHunter", "Progress")
				end
			end

			handlers2.Red = function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.Progress.Red < 1 then
					return arg2.Terrorshark()
				end
				return arg:CollectReward("Red")
			end

			handlers2.Yellow = function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.Progress.Yellow < 5 then
					return arg2.YellowQuest()
				end
				return arg:CollectReward("Yellow")
			end

			handlers2.Blue = function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.Progress.Blue >= 1 then
					return arg:CollectReward("Blue")
				end

				if localPlayer.Character then
					local tool = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

					if tool then
						tool = tool:FindFirstChild("Fruit") and tool.ToolTip ~= "Blox Fruit"
					end

					if tool then
						arg.Progress.Blue = 1
						return nil
					end
					local v_83, v_84, v_85 = ipairs(localPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren())

					while true do
						local v_86, v_87 = v_83(v_84, v_85)
						if v_86 == nil then
							break
						end

						if v_87:IsA("Tool") and v_87:FindFirstChild("Fruit") and v_87.ToolTip ~= "Blox Fruit" then
							arg.Progress.Blue = 1
							return nil
						end
						v_85 = v_86
					end

					return arg2.Fruits()
				end
			end

			handlers2.Black = function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.Progress.Black < 3 then
					if arg.BlackProgress then
						arg.Progress.Black = count["Dinosaur Bones"] - arg.BlackProgress
						return arg2.LavaGolem() or arg2.PrehistoricBones() or arg2.PrehistoricIsland()
					end
					arg.BlackProgress = arg.Progress.Black - count["Dinosaur Bones"]
					return true
				end

				return arg:CollectReward("Black")
			end

			tbl27.BeltProgress = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
				if handlers2.CurrentBelt ~= arg2 then
					return
				end
				handlers2.Progress[arg2] = handlers2.Progress[arg2] + arg3
			end

			tbl27.BeltQuest = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
				local beltName = arg2.BeltName

				if handlers2[beltName] then
					if unlocked["Dojo Belt (" .. beltName .. ")"] then
						return arg:GetNextBeltQuest()
					end

					if not (handlers2.Progress[beltName] and arg2.UpdatedProgress) then
						local progress = handlers2.Progress
						local progress2 = arg2.Progress
						arg2.UpdatedProgress = true
						progress[beltName] = progress2
					end

					handlers2.CurrentBelt = beltName
					if handlers2[beltName](handlers2, arg3) then
						return "Belt Quest: " .. beltName
					end
				end
			end

			tbl27.GetNextBeltQuest = function(arg)
				local v_83 = 5

				if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(cframe.Position) > v_83 then
					new(cframe)
				else
					arg.CurrentBeltQuest = rfInteractDragonQuest:InvokeServer(tbl29.DojoProgress)
				end

				return "Getting Belt Quest"
			end

			tbl27.BeltQuests = function(arg, arg2)
				local currentBeltQuest = arg.CurrentBeltQuest
				if type(currentBeltQuest) ~= "table" then
					return arg:GetNextBeltQuest()
				end

				if currentBeltQuest.Timeout or currentBeltQuest.Completed then
					return nil
				end

				if currentBeltQuest.Quest then
					return arg:BeltQuest(currentBeltQuest.Quest, arg2)
				end
			end

			tbl27.SpeakWizard = function(arg)
				local v_83 = 5

				if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(cframe2.Position) > v_83 then
					new(cframe2)
				else
					arg.CurrentDracoQuest = rfInteractDragonQuest:InvokeServer(tbl29.SpeakWizard)
				end

				return "Teleporting to NPC: Dragon Wizard"
			end

			tbl27.TalkNpc = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
				if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(arg2.Position) > 5 then
					new(arg2)
				elseif rfInteractDragonQuest:InvokeServer(arg3) and arg4 then
					arg[arg4] = nil
				end

				return "Teleporting to NPC: " .. (arg3.NPC or "???")
			end

			tbl27.GetDracoRace = function(arg, arg2)
				if not unlocked["Dojo Belt (Black)"] then
					return arg2.DojoTrainer()
				end
				local currentDracoQuest = arg.CurrentDracoQuest
				if type(currentDracoQuest) ~= "table" then
					return arg:SpeakWizard()
				end

				if not (currentDracoQuest.TetherLearned or currentDracoQuest.CanLearnTether) then
					return nil
				end

				if not currentDracoQuest.FoundPrehistoric then
					return arg2.PrehistoricBones() or arg2.PrehistoricEgg() or arg2.LavaGolem() or arg2.PrehistoricIsland()
				end

				if data.Race.Value ~= "Draco" then
					if currentDracoQuest.CanTransform or currentDracoQuest.CanTransformFree then
						return arg:TalkNpc(cframe2, tbl28.BuyDraco, "CurrentDracoQuest")
					end

					if currentDracoQuest.TetherLearned then
						if unlocked["Dragon Egg"] then
							return arg:TalkNpc(cframe2, tbl28.BuyDraco, "CurrentDracoQuest")
						end
						local v_83 = arg2.PrehistoricBones() or arg2.PrehistoricEgg()
						local v_84

						if v_83 then
							v_84 = v_83
						else
							v_84 = arg2.LavaGolem() or arg2.PrehistoricIsland()
						end

						return v_84
					end

					return arg:TalkNpc(cframe2, tbl28.LearnTether, "CurrentDracoQuest")
				end

				local availableVQuest = currentDracoQuest.AvailableVQuest
				if availableVQuest == "V2" or availableVQuest == "V3" then
					return arg:TalkNpc(cframe2, tbl28.Begin, "CurrentDracoQuest")
				end

				if availableVQuest == "V2InProgress" then
					if count["Fire Flower"] < 5 then
						return arg2.FireFlowers(5)
					end
					return arg:TalkNpc(cframe2, tbl28.Complete, "CurrentDracoQuest")
				end

				if availableVQuest ~= "V3InProgress" then
					if availableVQuest == "V2TurnInReady" then
						if data.Beli.Value <= 3000000 then
							return arg2.Level()
						end
						return arg:TalkNpc(cframe2, tbl28.Complete, "CurrentDracoQuest")
					end

					if availableVQuest == "V3TurnInReady" then
						if data.Beli.Value <= 3000000 then
							return arg2.Level()
						end
						return arg:TalkNpc(cframe2, tbl28.Complete, "CurrentDracoQuest")
					end

					return
				end

				return arg2.Terrorshark()
			end
		end

		return tbl27
	end

	managers.EspManager = function()
		local index = {}
		local tbl27 = {}
		index.__index = index

		index.__newindex = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			if arg2 == "Enabled" then
				return task.spawn(arg.toggle, arg, arg3)
			end
			return rawset(arg, arg2, arg3)
		end

		local function fn34(arg)
			if arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
				return arg.PrimaryPart or arg
			end

			if arg:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
				return arg.Handle
			end
			return arg
		end

		local function fn35(arg)
			if arg.Object and arg.Section.List[arg.Object] then
				arg.Section.List[arg.Object] = nil
			end

			local espHandle = arg.EspHandle

			if espHandle and espHandle.Parent then
				espHandle:Destroy()
			end

			arg.EspHandle = nil
		end

		index.new = function(name, instance, isEspObject, isEnabled)
			local obj = setmetatable({}, index)
			local folder2 = Instance.new("Folder", folder)
			folder2.Name = name
			obj.List = {}
			obj.Name = name
			obj.Folder = folder2
			obj.IsEnabled = isEnabled
			obj.Instance = instance
			obj.IsEspObject = isEspObject
			return obj
		end

		index.clear = function(arg)
			arg.Folder:ClearAllChildren()
			table.clear(arg.List)
		end

		index.add = function(arg, object, color, name)
			local tbl28 = { Section = arg }
			tbl28.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			tbl28.Name = name or object.Name
			tbl28.Object = object
			tbl28.EspHandle = nil
			local boxHandleAdornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
			boxHandleAdornment.Size = Vector3.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			boxHandleAdornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
			boxHandleAdornment.ZIndex = 10
			boxHandleAdornment.Transparency = 0
			local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
			billboardGui.Adornee = object
			billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 150)
			billboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
			billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
			local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -50)
			textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)
			textLabel.TextSize = 10
			textLabel.TextColor3 = tbl28.Color
			textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
			textLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom
			textLabel.Text = "..."
			textLabel.ZIndex = 15
			textLabel.RichText = true
			textLabel.Parent = billboardGui
			billboardGui.Parent = boxHandleAdornment
			boxHandleAdornment.Parent = arg.Folder
			tbl28.EspHandle = boxHandleAdornment

			task.spawn(function()
				local espHandle = tbl28.EspHandle
				local object2 = tbl28.Object

				while true do
					if not (rzSettings.SmoothMode and task.wait(0.25)) then
						game:GetService("RunService").Stepped:Wait()
					end

					if not object2 or not object2:IsDescendantOf(workspace) or not espHandle then
						return fn35(tbl28)
					end
					local pivot = fn34(object2)
					if not pivot then
						break
					end

					if pivot:IsA("Model") then
						pivot = pivot:GetPivot()
					end

					local n31 = math.floor(localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(pivot.Position) / 5)
					local humanoid = object2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					local isTool = object2:IsA("Tool") and object2 or object2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

					if humanoid then
						textLabel.Text = ("%s<font color='rgb(160, 160, 160)'> [ %im ]</font>\n<font color='rgb(25, 240, 25)'>[%i/%i]</font>"):format(tbl28.Name, n31, math.floor(humanoid.Health), math.floor(humanoid.MaxHealth))
					elseif object2.Parent ~= workspace and object2.Name ~= "Fruit " then
						textLabel.Text = ("%s < %i >"):format(tbl28.Name, n31)
					elseif object2.Parent == workspace and isTool then
						textLabel.Text = ("%s < %i >"):format(isTool.Name, n31)
					else
						textLabel.Text = ("%s < %i >"):format(lib2.FruitsName[object2] or object2.Name, n31)
					end
				end

				return fn35(tbl28)
			end)

			return tbl28
		end

		index.toggle = function(arg, arg2)
			local str9 = "Esp" .. arg.Name
			tbl27[str9] = arg2
			local isEnabled = arg.IsEnabled
			local instance = arg.Instance
			local isEspObject = arg.IsEspObject

			while tbl27[str9] do
				local v_83 = "table"
				local children

				if type(instance) ~= v_83 or not instance then
					children = instance:GetChildren()
				else
					children = instance
				end

				for i = 1, #children do
					local v_84 = children[i]

					if not arg.List[v_84] then
						local v_85, v_86, v_87, v_88 = isEspObject(v_84)

						if v_85 then
							arg.List[v_84] = arg:add(v_88 or v_84, v_86, v_87, isEnabled)
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.25)
			end

			arg:clear()
		end

		return index
	end

	managers.SeaManager = function()
		if lib2.GameData.Sea == 1 then
			return nil
		end

		local tbl27 = {
			oldTool = "Melee",
			SeaEvents = {},
			BoatTweenDebounce = 0,
			randomNumber = 1,
			toolDebounce = 0,
			rdDebounce = 0,
			nextNum = 1,
			DodgeVector = Vector3.new(0, 125, 0),
			nextTool = { Melee = "Blox Fruit", ["Blox Fruit"] = "Sword", Sword = "Gun", Gun = "Melee" },
		}

		tbl27.BuyBoat = {
			Position = lib2.GameData.Sea == 2 and CFrame.new(94, 10, 2951) or CFrame.new(-6123, 16, -2247),
			TikiIsland = CFrame.new(-16917, 9, 510),
		}

		local tbl28 = {}
		local tbl29 = {}
		local cframe = CFrame.new(-43, 21, 5054)
		local cframe2 = CFrame.new(1744, 21, 4393)
		local cframe3 = CFrame.new(1003, 21, 3598)
		local cframe4 = CFrame.new
		tbl29[1] = cframe
		tbl29[2] = cframe2
		tbl29[3] = cframe3

		do
			local values = table.pack(cframe4(-935, 21, 3813))
			table.move(values, 1, values.n, 4, tbl29)
		end

		tbl28[1] = false
		tbl28[2] = tbl29

		tbl28[3] = {
			inf = -100000000,
			["6"] = -43200,
			["5"] = -38200,
			["4"] = -34000,
			["3"] = -30000,
			["2"] = -26000,
			["1"] = -22000,
		}

		tbl27.RandomPosition = tbl28[lib2.GameData.Sea]
		local directions = {}
		local vector = Vector3.new(60, 0, 0)
		local vector2 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 60)
		local vector3 = Vector3.new(-60, 0, 0)
		local vector4 = Vector3.new(0, 0, -60)
		local vector5 = Vector3.new
		directions[1] = vector
		directions[2] = vector2
		directions[3] = vector3
		directions[4] = vector4

		do
			local values = table.pack(vector5(0, 0, 0))
			table.move(values, 1, values.n, 5, directions)
		end

		tbl27.Directions = directions
		local count = lib2.Inventory.Count
		local isAlive = lib2.IsAlive
		local equipTool = lib2.EquipTool
		local fireRemote = lib2.FireRemote
		local humanoid = nil
		local subclassNetwork = remotes:WaitForChild("SubclassNetwork")

		if lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 then
			local randomPosition = tbl27.RandomPosition
			local v_83, v_84, v_85 = pairs(randomPosition)

			while true do
				local v_86, v_87 = v_83(v_84, v_85)

				if v_86 ~= nil then
					local tbl30 = {}
					local cframe5 = CFrame.new(v_87, 21, 500)
					local cframe6 = CFrame.new(v_87 - 1500, 21, 500)
					local cframe7 = CFrame.new(v_87 - 3000, 21, 2000)
					local cframe8 = CFrame.new
					tbl30[1] = cframe5
					tbl30[2] = cframe6
					tbl30[3] = cframe7

					do
						local values = table.pack(cframe8(v_87, 21, -1000))
						table.move(values, 1, values.n, 4, tbl30)
					end

					randomPosition[v_86] = tbl30
					v_85 = v_86
					continue
				end

				break
			end
		end

		tbl27.IsOwner = function(arg)
			if _G.AutoBoatAnother then
				Player = _G.SelectPlayerBoat
			else
				Player = localPlayer.Name
			end

			return arg:FindFirstChild("Owner") and arg.Owner.Value.Name == Player
		end

		tbl27.SeaAlive = function(arg)
			local health = arg:FindFirstChild("Health")
			if not health then
				return true
			end

			if health:IsA("NumberValue") or health:IsA("IntValue") then
				return health.Value > 0
			end

			if health:IsA("Humanoid") then
				return health.Health > 0
			end
			return false
		end

		tbl27.GetPlayerBoat = function()
			local name = localPlayer.Name

			if _G.AutoBoatAnother then
				name = _G.SelectPlayerBoat
			end

			local v_83 = next
			local children, v_84 = game:GetService("Workspace").Boats:GetChildren()

			for _, v_85 in v_83, children, v_84 do
				if v_85:IsA("Model") and v_85:FindFirstChild("Owner") and tostring(v_85.Owner.Value) == name and v_85:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v_85.Humanoid.Value > 0 then
					_G.PlayerBoat = v_85
					return v_85
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		tbl27.BuyNewBoat = function(arg)
			local buyBoat = arg.BuyBoat
			local position = buyBoat.Position

			if lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 then
				position = buyBoat.TikiIsland
			end

			if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(position.Position) >= 10 then
				new(position)
			else
				fireRemote("BuyBoat", _G.SelectBoatUse)
			end
		end

		tbl27.BuyBeast = function(arg)
			local buyBoat = arg.BuyBoat
			local position = buyBoat.Position

			if lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 then
				position = buyBoat.TikiIsland
			end

			if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(position.Position) >= 10 then
				new(position)
			else
				fireRemote("BuyBoat", "Beast Hunter")
			end
		end

		tbl27.teleportBoat = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local n31

			if _G.FastBoatSpeed then
				n31 = 700
			else
				n31 = 400
			end

			local boatTweenDebounce = arg.BoatTweenDebounce

			if tick() - boatTweenDebounce >= 0.5 then
				lib2.Tween.Velocity = (arg3.Position - arg2.Position).Unit * n31
				lib2:RemoveBoatCollision(arg2.Parent)
				arg.BoatTweenDebounce = tick()
			end
		end

		tbl27.StopBoat = function()
			lib2.Tween.Velocity = Vector3.zero
		end

		tbl27.GetSelectedLevel = function(arg, arg2)
			return arg.RandomPosition[arg2 or rzSettings.SeaLevel]
		end

		tbl27.UseBoatTP = function(arg, arg2)
			if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
				humanoid = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				return nil
			end

			if not humanoid.SeatPart then
				return arg:TeleportToBoat()
			end
			local primaryPart = arg:GetPlayerBoat().PrimaryPart
			if not primaryPart then
				return nil
			end

			for _, descendant in pairs(game.Workspace.Boats:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			for _, descendant in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			arg:teleportBoat(primaryPart, arg2)
		end

		tbl27.RandomTeleport = function(arg, arg2)
			if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
				humanoid = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				return nil
			end

			if not humanoid.SeatPart then
				return arg:TeleportToBoat()
			end
			local primaryPart = arg:GetPlayerBoat().PrimaryPart
			if not primaryPart then
				return nil
			end

			if humanoid.SeatPart and _G.FlyBoat then
				primaryPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(primaryPart.Position.X, rzSettings.BoatNewHeight, primaryPart.Position.Z)
			end

			for _, descendant in pairs(game.Workspace.Boats:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			for _, descendant in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			local position = primaryPart.Position
			local selectedLevel = lib2.GameData.Sea == 3 and arg:GetSelectedLevel(arg2) or arg.RandomPosition

			if #selectedLevel ~= 1 then
				if #selectedLevel > 1 then
					if #selectedLevel < arg.nextNum then
						arg.nextNum = 1
					end

					local v_83 = selectedLevel[arg.nextNum]

					if rzSettings.BoatNewHeight <= (position - v_83.Position).Magnitude then
						arg:teleportBoat(primaryPart, v_83)
					else
						arg.nextNum = arg.nextNum + 1
					end
				end

				if not flag2 then
					return
				end
			else
				arg:teleportBoat(primaryPart, selectedLevel[1])
			end
		end

		tbl27.RandomTool = function(arg)
			local toolDebounce = arg.toolDebounce
			if tick() - toolDebounce < 2 then
				return arg.oldTool
			end
			arg.toolDebounce = tick()
			local v_83 = arg.nextTool[arg.oldTool]
			local v_84 = 0

			while not VerifyToolTip(v_83) do
				v_83 = arg.nextTool[v_83]
				v_84 += 1
				if not (v_84 >= 3) then
					continue
				end
				arg.oldTool = v_83
				return v_83
			end

			arg.oldTool = v_83
			return v_83
		end

		tbl27.GetSeaEvent = function(arg, arg2)
			local v_83, v_84, v_85 = ipairs(enemies:GetChildren())
			local v_86

			while true do
				if true then
					local v_87
					v_87, v_86 = v_83(v_84, v_85)
					if v_87 == nil then
						return
					end

					if v_86.Name == arg2 and isAlive(v_86) then
						if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(v_86:GetPivot().Position) <= 2000 then
							break
						end
						v_85 = v_87
						continue
					end

					v_85 = v_87
				else
					return
				end
			end

			return v_86
		end

		tbl27.GetBoat = function(arg, arg2)
			local v_83, v_84, v_85 = ipairs(enemies:GetChildren())
			local v_86

			while true do
				local v_87
				v_87, v_86 = v_83(v_84, v_85)

				if v_87 == nil then
					return
				else
					if v_86.Name == arg2 and v_86:FindFirstChild("Health") and v_86.Health.Value > 0 then
						if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(v_86:GetPivot().Position) <= 2000 then
							break
						end
						v_85 = v_87
						continue
					end

					v_85 = v_87
				end
			end

			return v_86
		end

		tbl27.attackBoat = function(arg, arg2)
			local primaryPart = arg2.PrimaryPart

			if primaryPart and arg2:FindFirstChild("Health") and arg2.Health.Value > 0 then
				if arg2.Name == "PirateGrandBrigade" then
					v517 = primaryPart.Parent.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -_G.BoatGrand, -15)
				elseif arg2.Name == "FishBoat" then
					v517 = primaryPart.Parent.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -_G.BoatFish, -15)
				elseif arg2.Name == "GhostShip" then
					v517 = primaryPart.Parent.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -_G.BoatGhost, -15)
				else
					v517 = primaryPart.Parent.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -_G.Boat, -15)
				end

				EnableBuso()
				new(v517)
				arg:StopBoat()

				if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(v517.Position) < 50 then
					UseSkills(primaryPart, _G.SelectSkill)

					if _G.UseGun then
						equipTool("Gun", true)
					elseif _G.UseFruit then
						equipTool("Blox Fruit", true)
					else
						equipTool(tbl27:RandomTool(), true)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		tbl27.attackFish = function(arg, arg2)
			local primaryPart = arg2.PrimaryPart

			if primaryPart then
				if _G.DodgeShark then
					if arg2.Name == "Terrorshark" and worldOrigin:FindFirstChild("SpinSlash4") or worldOrigin:FindFirstChild("SpinSlash3") or worldOrigin:FindFirstChild("SpinSlash") or worldOrigin:FindFirstChild("GroundExplosionSplashStart") or worldOrigin:FindFirstChild("FinalSpinAttachment") then
						new(primaryPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 400, 0))
					else
						new(primaryPart.CFrame + rzSettings.FarmPos)
					end
				else
					new(primaryPart.CFrame + rzSettings.FarmPos)
				end

				if arg2.Name == "Terrorshark" then
					UseSkills(primaryPart, _G.SelectSkill)
				end

				equipTool()
				EnableBuso()
				arg:StopBoat()
			end
		end

		tbl27.StartHolding = function(arg, arg2)
			if not arg2:GetAttribute("Repairing") then
				VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
				print("Segurando o mouse")
				arg2.AncestryChanged:Wait()
				VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
				print("Parou de segurar")
			end
		end

		tbl27.RepairBoat = function(arg, arg2)
			local humanoid2 = rzSettings.RepairBoat and subclass.Value == "Shipwright" and count["Wooden Plank"] > 0 and arg2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			local attribute

			if humanoid2 then
				attribute = arg2:GetAttribute("__Repair")

				if not attribute then
					attribute = humanoid2.Value < (arg2:GetAttribute("MaxHealth") or humanoid2.Value) / 1.2
				end
			else
				attribute = humanoid2
			end

			if attribute then
				local repairHammer = (localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()):FindFirstChild("_RepairHammer")
				local value = humanoid2.Value

				if (arg2:GetAttribute("MaxHealth") or humanoid2.Value) <= value then
					arg2:SetAttribute("__Repair", nil)
				else
					arg2:SetAttribute("__Repair", true)
				end

				if not (repairHammer and repairHammer:WaitForChild("Marker")) then
					if arg2:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
						local n31 = arg2.VehicleSeat.CFrame + Vector3.yAxis * 20

						if 5 < localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(n31.Position) then
							new(n31)
						else
							subclassNetwork.UseSubclass:InvokeServer({ Action = "RequestHammer" })
						end
					end

					return true, arg:StopBoat(arg2)
				end

				new(repairHammer.Marker.Value.WorldCFrame + Vector3.xAxis * 10)
				task.spawn(arg.StartHolding, arg, repairHammer)
				return true
			end

			local flag14 = subclass.Value == "Shipwright"
			local character

			if flag14 then
				character = rzSettings.RepairBoat and localPlayer.Character
			else
				character = flag14
			end

			if character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("_RepairHammer") then
				subclassNetwork.UseSubclass:InvokeServer({ Action = "RequestHammer" })
			end
		end

		tbl27.attackSeaEvent = function(arg, arg2)
			if arg2:GetAttribute("IsBoat") then
				arg:attackBoat(arg2)
			else
				arg:attackFish(arg2)
			end
		end

		tbl27.RandomDirection = function(arg)
			local rdDebounce = arg.rdDebounce
			if tick() - rdDebounce < 1.5 then
				return arg.Directions[arg.randomNumber]
			end
			arg.rdDebounce = tick()
			arg.randomNumber = math.random(#arg.Directions)
			return arg.Directions[arg.randomNumber]
		end

		tbl27.attackSeaBeast = function(arg, arg2)
			arg:RandomDirection()
			local v_83 = arg2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not v_83 then
				return nil
			end
			local position = v_83.Position
			local distanceSeaBeast = _G.DistanceSeaBeast
			local z = position.Z
			local cframe5 = CFrame.new(position.X, map:WaitForChild("WaterBase-Plane", 9000000000.0).Position.Y + distanceSeaBeast, z)
			EnableBuso()

			if _G.DodgeSea then
				local waterBasePlane = map:FindFirstChild("WaterBase-Plane")

				if waterBasePlane and worldOrigin:FindFirstChild("LightBullet") then
					local y = waterBasePlane.Position.Y
					local tbl30 = {}
					local vector6 = Vector3.new(0, 1300, 0)
					local vector7 = Vector3.new(500, 1200, 0)
					local vector8 = Vector3.new(-500, 1000, 300)
					local vector9 = Vector3.new(300, 800, -400)
					local vector10 = Vector3.new
					local v_84 = 600
					tbl30[1] = vector6
					tbl30[2] = vector7
					tbl30[3] = vector8
					tbl30[4] = vector9

					do
						local values = table.pack(vector10(-400, v_84, -300))
						table.move(values, 1, values.n, 5, tbl30)
					end

					for _, v_85 in ipairs(tbl30) do
						new(CFrame.new(position.X + v_85.X, y + v_85.Y, position.Z + v_85.Z))
						task.wait(0.2)
					end
				else
					new(cframe5)
				end
			else
				new(cframe5)
			end

			arg:StopBoat()

			if _G.UseGun then
				equipTool("Gun", true)
			elseif _G.UseFruit then
				equipTool("Blox Fruit", true)
			else
				equipTool(tbl27:RandomTool(), true)
			end

			UseSkills(v_83, _G.SelectSkill)
		end

		tbl27.GetSeaBeast = function(arg)
			local seaBeast = arg.SeaBeast
			if seaBeast and seaBeast.Parent == seaBeasts and isAlive(seaBeast) then
				return seaBeast
			end
			local v_83, v_84, v_85 = ipairs(seaBeasts:GetChildren())
			local n31 = 2000
			local v_86 = nil

			while true do
				local v_87, v_88 = v_83(v_84, v_85)

				if v_87 == nil then
					break
				elseif v_88:IsA("Model") then
					local v_89 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(v_88:GetPivot().Position)

					if isAlive(v_88) then
						if v_89 < n31 then
							n31 = v_89
							v_86 = v_88
							v_85 = v_87
						else
							v_85 = v_87
						end
					else
						v_85 = v_87
					end
				else
					v_85 = v_87
				end
			end

			arg.SeaBeast = v_86
			return v_86
		end

		tbl27.TeleportToBoat = function(arg)
			if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not humanoid:IsDescendantOf(characters) then
				humanoid = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				return nil
			end
			local vehicleSeat = arg.VehicleSeat

			if vehicleSeat and vehicleSeat:IsDescendantOf(_G.PlayerBoat) then
				if humanoid.SeatPart and humanoid.SeatPart ~= vehicleSeat then
					humanoid.Sit = false
				elseif localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(vehicleSeat.Position) >= 100 then
					new(vehicleSeat.CFrame)
				else
					vehicleSeat:Sit(humanoid)
				end

				task.wait(0.25)
			elseif _G.PlayerBoat then
				arg.VehicleSeat = _G.PlayerBoat:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
			end
		end

		return tbl27
	end

	managers.FruitManager = function()
		local isAlive = lib2.IsAlive
		local count = lib2.Inventory.Count

		return {
			RandomDebounce = 0,
			MoneyToReroll = 0,
			GetRealFruitName = function(arg, arg2)
				local v_83 = string.gsub(arg2.Name, " Fruit", "")
				return v_83 .. "-" .. v_83
			end,
			CanStoreFruit = function(arg, arg2)
				return count[arg:GetRealFruitName(arg2)] < fruitCap.Value
			end,
			StoreFruit = function(arg, arg2)
				return lib2.FireRemote("StoreFruit", arg:GetRealFruitName(arg2), arg2)
			end,
			GetRealFruitSkinName = function(arg, arg2)
				return (string.gsub(arg2.Name, " Fruit", ""))
			end,
			CanSkinStoreFruit = function(arg, arg2)
				return count[arg:GetRealFruitSkinName(arg2)] < fruitCap.Value
			end,
			StoreSkinFruit = function(arg, arg2)
				return lib2.FireRemote("StoreFruit", arg:GetRealFruitSkinName(arg2), arg2)
			end,
			IsFruit = function(arg, arg2)
				local flag14

				if string.sub(arg2.Name, -6, -1) ~= " Fruit" then
					flag14 = false
				else
					flag14 = arg2:GetAttribute("DroppedBy")
				end

				return flag14
			end,
			GetInventoryItems = function()
				local children = localPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()
				local tool = localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

				if tool then
					table.insert(children, tool)
				end

				return children
			end,
			CanBuyMicrochip = function(arg)
				if not isAlive(localPlayer.Character) then
					return false
				end

				if localPlayer:GetAttribute("IslandRaiding") then
					return false
				end

				if localPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Microchip") or localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Microchip") then
					return false
				end
				local v_83, v_84, v_85 = ipairs(arg:GetInventoryItems())

				while true do
					local v_86, v_87 = v_83(v_84, v_85)

					if v_86 == nil then
						return -1
					else
						if v_87:IsA("Tool") and arg:IsFruit(v_87) then
							break
						end
						v_85 = v_86
					end
				end

				return true
			end,
			GetStorableFruit = function(arg, arg2)
				if not isAlive(localPlayer.Character) then
					return false
				end
				local v_83, v_84, v_85 = ipairs(arg:GetInventoryItems())

				while true do
					local v_86, v_87 = v_83(v_84, v_85)
					if not (v_86 == nil or v_87.Name ~= arg2 and not isAlive(localPlayer.Character)) then
						v_85 = v_86
						continue
					end
					break
				end
			end,
		}
	end

	handlers.RunModules = function(arg)
		local v_83 = next
		local managers2 = arg.Managers
		local v_84 = nil

		while true do
			local v_85, v_86 = v_83(managers2, v_84)

			if v_85 == nil then
				break
			else
				local ok, result = pcall(v_86)

				if ok then
					arg.Managers[v_85] = result
					env[v_85] = result
					v_84 = v_85
				else
					env[v_85] = nil
					warn("falha ao carregar Module [ omg hub ]: " .. v_85 .. " : " .. result)
					v_84 = v_85
				end
			end
		end
	end

	UseSkills = function(arg, arg2)
		if not arg or not arg.Position then
			return
		end

		if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(arg.Position) > 500 then
			return
		end
		local VirtualInputManager2 = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

		for k, v_83 in pairs(arg2) do
			if v_83 then
				VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(true, k, false, game)
				task.wait(0.05)
				VirtualInputManager2:SendKeyEvent(false, k, false, game)

				if _G.AimbotInFarm then
					if lib2 and lib2.Hooking then
						lib2.Hooking:SetTarget(arg, true)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	CanBuyMelee = function(arg)
		if VerifyTool(arg) then
			return false, "Alr Equipped"
		end

		if pricemelee[arg] then
			if beli.Value < pricemelee[arg].Beli then
				return false, "Need Money"
			end

			if pricemelee[arg].Fragment > fragments.Value then
				return false, "Need Fragment"
			end
		end

		if arg == "Superhuman" then
			for _, v_83 in pairs({ "Black Leg", "Fishman Karate", "Electro", "Dragon Claw" }) do
				if not VerifyTool(v_83) then
					return false, "Need " .. v_83
				end

				if GetToolMastery(v_83) < 300 then
					return false, "Need Mastery " .. v_83
				end
			end
		end

		if arg == "Death Step" then
			if not VerifyTool("Black Leg") or GetToolMastery("Black Leg") < 400 then
				return false, "Need Black Leg Mastery 400"
			end
		end

		if arg == "Sharkman Karate" then
			if not VerifyTool("Fishman Karate") or GetToolMastery("Fishman Karate") < 400 then
				return false, "Need Fishman Mastery 400"
			end
		end

		if arg == "Electric Claw" then
			if not VerifyTool("Electro") or GetToolMastery("Electro") < 400 then
				return false, "Need Electro Mastery 400"
			end
		end

		if arg == "Dragon Talon" then
			local BuyDragonTalon_ = vu897("BuyDragonTalon", true)
			Used_Fire_Essence = BuyDragonTalon_ == 0 or BuyDragonTalon_ == 3
			if not Used_Fire_Essence then
				return false, "Need Fire Essense"
			end
		end

		if arg == "Godhuman" then
			for _, v_83 in pairs({ "Superhuman", "Death Step", "Sharkman Karate", "Electric Claw", "Dragon Talon" }) do
				if not VerifyTool(v_83) then
					return false, "Need " .. v_83
				end

				if GetToolMastery(v_83) < 400 then
					return false, "Mastery insufficient" .. v_83
				end
			end
		end

		return true
	end

		local function fn34(arg2, arg3)
			if not arg2 or arg2 == "" then
				return nil
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return http.request({
					Url = string.format("https://discord.com/api/v9/invites/%s?with_counts=true&with_expiration=true", arg2),
					Method = "GET",
					Headers = { ["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0" },
				})
			end)

			if not ok or not result or result.StatusCode ~= 200 then
				return nil
			end

			local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
				return HttpService:JSONDecode(result.Body)
			end)

			if not ok2 or not result2 or not result2.guild then
				return nil
			end
			local guild = result2.guild

				Icon = guild.icon,
				IconURL = str9,
				NSFW = guild.nsfw or false,
				CreatedAt = result2.created_at or nil,
			}

			if arg3 == nil or arg3 == "All" then
				return tbl27
			end

			if tbl27[arg3] ~= nil then
				return tbl27[arg3]
			end
			return nil

							local v_90, v_91 = v_87(v_88, v_89)

							if v_90 ~= nil then
								if v_91 and stats2[v_90].Level.Value < lib2.GameData.MaxLevel then
									local clamp = math.clamp
									local clamp2 = math.clamp
									local statsPoints = rzSettings.StatsPoints or 3
									local maxLevel = lib2.GameData.MaxLevel
									local v_92 = clamp(clamp2(statsPoints, 0, value), 0, maxLevel)
									fireRemote("AddPoint", v_90, v_92)
									v_89 = v_90
								else
									v_89 = v_90
								end

								continue
							end

							break
						end
					end
				end
			end

		local function fn40(arg2)

			local function fn42()
				local playerGui = localPlayer.PlayerGui

				for _, v_90 in ipairs(tbl31) do
					for _, child in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "ScreenGui" and child:FindFirstChild("1") then
							local n210 = child["1"]:FindFirstChild("2")
							if n210 and n210:FindFirstChild("DisplayName") and n210.DisplayName.Text:find(v_90) then
								firesignal(n210.Activated)
								return
							end
							firesignal(n210.Activated)
							return
						end
					end
				end
			end

		local isAlive = lib2.IsAlive

		CheckIsAlly = function(arg2)
			if not arg2 then
				return false
			end

			if arg2:IsA("Player") and tostring(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Team) == "Marines" and tostring(arg2.Team) == "Marines" then
				return true
			end

			if arg2 == game.Players.LocalPlayer then
				return true
			end

			if game:GetService("CollectionService"):HasTag(arg2, "Ally" .. game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name) or game:GetService("CollectionService"):HasTag(game.Players.LocalPlayer, "Ally" .. arg2.Name) then
				return true
			end
			return false
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			while task.wait() do
				if _G.AimbotNearest then
					if isAlive(localPlayer.Character) then
						local Mouse = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Mouse)
						local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local huge = math.huge
						local v_90 = nil

						if not _G.IgnoreEnemies then
							v_90 = nil

							for _, child in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
								local v_91 = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
								local head = child:FindFirstChild("Head")

								if v_91 and v_91.Health > 0 and head then
									local magnitude = (head.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

									if magnitude < huge then
										huge = magnitude
										v_90 = head
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if not _G.IgnorePlayers then
							for _, player in pairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if player ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer and player.Character and not CheckIsAlly(player) then
									local v_91 = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
									local head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

									if v_91 and v_91.Health > 0 and head then
										local magnitude = (head.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

										if magnitude < huge then
											huge = magnitude
											v_90 = head
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if v_90 then
							Mouse.Hit = v_90.CFrame
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		local function fn43()
			if not ok or not result then
				return math.random(1, 1e40), 1
			end
			local v_97 = getupvalue(result, 13)
			local v_98 = getupvalue(result, 14)
			local v_99 = getupvalue(result, 15)
			local v_100 = getupvalue(result, 16)
			local v_101 = getupvalue(result, 17)
			local v_102 = getupvalue(result, 18)
			local v_103 = getupvalue(result, 19)
			local n33 = ((v_99 * v_98 + v_97 * v_100) % v_101 * v_101 + v_97 * v_98) % v_102
			local n34 = math.floor(n33 / v_101)
			local n35 = v_103 + 1
			setupvalue(result, 13, n33 - n34 * v_101)
			setupvalue(result, 15, n34)
			setupvalue(result, 19, n35)
			return math.floor(n33 / v_102 * 16777215), n35
		end

		local function fn44(arg2)
			arg2 = arg2 and arg2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			return arg2 and arg2.Health > 0
		end

		local function fn45(arg2)
			for i = 1, 6 do
				local v_97 = arg2:FindFirstChild(rzFunctions2.HitboxLimbs[math.random(#rzFunctions2.HitboxLimbs)])
				if v_97 and v_97:IsA("BasePart") then
					return v_97
				end
			end

			return arg2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		end

		local function fn46(arg2)
			local humanoidRootPart = arg2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart then
				return {}
			end
			local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
			local tbl32 = {}

			local function fn47(arg3)
				if not arg3 then
					return
				end

				for _, child in ipairs(arg3:GetChildren()) do
					if child ~= arg2 and fn44(child) then
						local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if humanoidRootPart2 and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude <= rzFunctions2.Range then
							tbl32[#tbl32 + 1] = child
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if rzFunctions2.AttackMobs then
				fn47(workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies"))
				fn47(workspace:FindFirstChild("SeaBeasts"))
			end

			if rzFunctions2.AttackPlayers then
				fn47(workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters"))
			end

			return tbl32
		end

		local function fn47(arg2)
			local tbl32 = {}

			for _, v_97 in ipairs(arg2) do
				local v_98 = fn45(v_97)

				if v_98 then
					tbl32[#tbl32 + 1] = { v_97, v_98 }
				end
			end

			return tbl32
		end

		local function fn48(arg2)
			local v_97 = fn47(arg2)
			if #v_97 == 0 then
				return
			end
			local head = v_97[1][1]:FindFirstChild("Head") or v_97[1][1]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if not head then

				return
			end

			reRegisterAttack:FireServer(0)
			reRegisterHit:FireServer(head, v_97)
		end

		local function fn49(arg2)
			if not (rzFunctions2.UseObfuscated and v_96 and attribute) then
				return
			end
			local v_97 = fn47(arg2)
			if #v_97 == 0 then
				return
			end
			local head = v_97[1][1]:FindFirstChild("Head") or v_97[1][1]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not head then
