			end

			pcall(function()
				reRegisterAttack:FireServer()

				local RegisterHit = string.gsub("RE/RegisterHit", ".", function(arg3)
					local floor2 = math.floor
					local v_98 = workspace
					return string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(arg3), floor2(v_98:GetServerTimeNow() / 10 % 10) + 1))
				end)

				local v_98 = 2
				local v_99 = bit32.bxor(attribute + 909090, net2.seed:InvokeServer() * v_98)
				cloneref(v_96):FireServer(RegisterHit, v_99, head, v_97)
			end)
		end

		local function fn50(arg2)
			if not (rzFunctions2.ResetCooldown and arg2) then
				return
			end

			pcall(function()
				for _, descendant in ipairs(arg2:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("NumberValue") then
						local str9 = descendant.Name:lower()

						if str9:find("cooldown") or str9:find("cd") or str9:find("reload") then
							descendant.Value = 0
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		local tbl32 = {}

		local function fn51(arg2)
			if not rzFunctions2.Paralyze or not arg2 or not arg2.Parent then
				return
			end

			if game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(arg2) then
				return
			end
			local humanoidRootPart = arg2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local v_97 = arg2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not humanoidRootPart or not v_97 then
				return
			end
			local v_98 = tbl32[arg2]
			local pos = v_98 and v_98.pos or humanoidRootPart.Position

			if not pos then
				tbl32[arg2] = { pos = pos }
			end

			pcall(function()
				if setnetworkowner then
					setnetworkowner(arg2, false)
				end
			end)

			pcall(function()
				humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, pos + humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector)
			end)

			pcall(function()
				v_97.WalkSpeed = 0
				v_97.JumpPower = 0
				v_97.JumpHeight = 0
				v_97.AutoRotate = false
			end)

			pcall(function()
				local rzLock = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("rzLock")

				if not rzLock then
					local bodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
					bodyPosition.Name = "rzLock"
					bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
					bodyPosition.P = 200000
					bodyPosition.D = 1000
					bodyPosition.Position = pos
					bodyPosition.Parent = humanoidRootPart
				else
					rzLock.Position = pos
				end

				local rzGyro = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("rzGyro")

				if not rzGyro then
					local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
					bodyGyro.Name = "rzGyro"
					bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
					bodyGyro.P = 200000
					bodyGyro.D = 1000
					bodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, pos + humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector)
					bodyGyro.Parent = humanoidRootPart
				else
					rzGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, pos + humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector)
				end
			end)
		end

		local function fn52(arg2)
			if not arg2 then
				return
			end
			local v_97 = arg2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			tbl32[arg2] = nil
		end

		end

		local v_97 = 0

		task.spawn(function()
			while task.wait(rzFunctions2.LoopDelay) do
				if rzFunctions2.Enabled then
					local character = localPlayer2.Character

					if not (not character or not fn44(character)) then
						local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

						if tool then
							local toolTip = tool.ToolTip
							local now3 = os.clock()

							if toolTip == "Melee" or toolTip == "Sword" then
								if rzFunctions2.MeleeDelay <= now3 - v_97 then
									local v_98 = fn46(character)

									if 0 < #v_98 then
										v_97 = now3
										pcall(fn53, character, tool, v_98)
									end
								end
							elseif toolTip == "Blox Fruit" then
								local v_98 = fn46(character)

								if #v_98 > 0 then
									pcall(fn54, character, tool, v_98)
								end
							elseif toolTip == "Gun" then
								local v_98 = fn46(character)

								if 0 < #v_98 then
									pcall(fn55, character, tool, v_98)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		task.spawn(function()
			while task.wait(rzFunctions2.ParalyzeTick) do
				if not (not rzFunctions2.Enabled or not rzFunctions2.Paralyze) then
					local character = localPlayer2.Character

					if character then
						local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

						if tool then
							local toolTip = tool.ToolTip

							if not (toolTip ~= "Melee" and toolTip ~= "Sword" and toolTip ~= "Gun" and toolTip ~= "Blox Fruit") then
								local v_98 = fn46(character)

								for _, v_99 in ipairs(v_98) do
									fn51(v_99)
								end

								for k in pairs(tbl32) do
									if not k.Parent or not fn44(k) then
										fn52(k)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)

		local function createScreenGui()
			if playerGui:FindFirstChild("BlackOMG") then
				return playerGui.BlackOMG
			end
			local screenGui2 = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			screenGui2.Name = "BlackOMG"
			screenGui2.ResetOnSpawn = false
			screenGui2.IgnoreGuiInset = true
			screenGui2.DisplayOrder = 999999
			screenGui2.Parent = playerGui
			local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
			frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
			frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			frame.Parent = screenGui2
			local frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
			frame2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 300)
			frame2.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			frame2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			frame2.Parent = frame
			local uiListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
			uiListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
			uiListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
			uiListLayout.Parent = frame2

			local function fn56(name2)
				local textLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				textLabel2.Name = name2
				textLabel2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 40)
				textLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				textLabel2.TextSize = 20
				textLabel2.Text = ""
				textLabel2.Parent = frame2
			end

			fn56("NameLabel")
			fn56("LevelLabel")
			fn56("GemsLabel")
			fn56("GoldLabel")
			fn56("HolidayLabel")
			fn56("CandyLabel")
			return screenGui2
		end

		local function fn56()
			if _G.FpsBoost then
				if not BoostAlr then
					BoostAlr = true
					local v_99 = game
					local lighting = v_99.Lighting
					local terrain = v_99.Workspace.Terrain
					terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
					terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
					terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
					terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
					lighting.GlobalShadows = false
					lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
					lighting.Brightness = 0
					settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"

					for _, descendant in pairs(v_99:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("Part") or descendant:IsA("Union") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
							descendant.Material = "Plastic"
							descendant.Reflectance = 0
						elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") then
							descendant.Transparency = 1
						elseif descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") then
							descendant.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn38(arg2, gettingBossQuest, arg3)
			local quest = arg2.Quest
			local bossQuest

			if quest then
				local flag14 = arg3 == false

				if flag14 then
					bossQuest = flag14
				else
					bossQuest = not arg3 and rzSettings.BossQuest
				end
			else
				bossQuest = quest
			end

			if bossQuest and level.Value >= arg2.Level and not questManager:VerifyQuest(gettingBossQuest) then
				questManager:StartQuest(quest[1], quest[3] or 3, quest[2])
				return "Getting Boss Quest: " .. gettingBossQuest
			end
			local v_85 = enemySpawned(gettingBossQuest)
			if v_85 and v_85.PrimaryPart then
				return "Killing: " .. gettingBossQuest, attack(v_85)
			end

			if arg2.Position then
				return "Waiting for: " .. gettingBossQuest, new(arg2.Position)
			end
		end

							huge = v_90
						end
					else

					end
				end
			end

			return part
		end

				end
			end
		end

		fn34("KillPlayersV4", function()
			local Workspace_ = game:GetService("Workspace")
			local Players2 = game:GetService("Players")
			local localPlayer2 = Players2.LocalPlayer
			local character = localPlayer2.Character
			character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not character then
				return
			end

			if Workspace_.Map["Temple of Time"].FFABorder.Forcefield.Transparency < 1 and localPlayer2.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
				for _, player in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
					if player ~= localPlayer2 then
						local character2 = player.Character
						local humanoid = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
						local humanoidRootPart = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if humanoid and humanoidRootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 then
							if (humanoidRootPart.Position - character.Position).Magnitude <= 300 then
								attack(character2)
								return true
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end, sea == 3)

		GetSeaBeastTrial = function()
			if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("FishmanTrial") then
				return nil
			end

			if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water") then
				FishmanTrial = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water")
			end

			if FishmanTrial then
				local v_85 = next
				local children, v_86 = workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

				for _, v_87 in v_85, children, v_86 do
					if v_87:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (v_87.HumanoidRootPart.Position - FishmanTrial.Position).Magnitude <= 1500 then
						if v_87.Health.Value > 0 then
							return v_87
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn44(arg2, arg3)
			local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
			if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				return false
			end
			return (character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - arg2).Magnitude <= arg3
		end

		fn34("TrialV4", function()
			local localPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
			if not localPlayer2 then
				return
			end

			if not localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
				return
			end

			if not localPlayer2.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Main") then
				return
			end

			if not localPlayer2.PlayerGui.Main:FindFirstChild("TopHUDList") then
				return
			end

			if not localPlayer2.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList:FindFirstChild("RaidTimer") then
				return
			end

			if localPlayer2.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible == true then
				if not localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Data") then
					return
				end

				if not localPlayer2.Data:FindFirstChild("Race") then
					return
				end
				local str9 = tostring(localPlayer2.Data.Race.Value)

				if str9 == "Mink" then
					if workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("MinkTrial") and workspace.Map.MinkTrial:FindFirstChild("Ceiling") then
						if fn44(workspace.Map.MinkTrial.Ceiling.Position, 5000) then
							new(workspace.Map.MinkTrial.Ceiling.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -20, 0))
							return true
						end
					end
				elseif str9 == "Cyborg" then
					if workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("CyborgTrial") and workspace.Map.CyborgTrial:FindFirstChild("Floor") then
						if fn44(workspace.Map.CyborgTrial.Floor.Position, 5000) then
							new(CFrame.new(-5119, 315, -2964))
							return true
						end
					end
				elseif str9 == "Skypiea" then
					if workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("SkyTrial") and workspace.Map.SkyTrial:FindFirstChild("Model") and workspace.Map.SkyTrial.Model:FindFirstChild("FinishPart") then
						if fn44(workspace.Map.SkyTrial.Model.FinishPart.Position, 5000) then
							new(workspace.Map.SkyTrial.Model.FinishPart.CFrame)
							return true
						end
					end
				elseif str9 == "Human" then
					if workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies") and localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
								if child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (localPlayer2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 500 then
									attack(child)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				elseif str9 == "Fishman" then
					local v_85 = GetSeaBeastTrial()
					local localPlayer3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
					local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer3.Character and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if v_85 and humanoidRootPart and v_85:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						if (humanoidRootPart.Position - v_85.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1500 then
							local z = v_85.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z
							new(CFrame.new(v_85.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, map:WaitForChild("WaterBase-Plane").Position.Y + 300, z))
							local z2 = v_85.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z
							local selectSkill = _G.SelectSkill
							UseSkills(CFrame.new(v_85.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, map:WaitForChild("WaterBase-Plane").Position.Y + 300, z2), selectSkill)
							EnableBuso()

							if _G.UseGun then
								equipTool("Gun", true)
							elseif _G.UseFruit then
								equipTool("Blox Fruit", true)
							else
								local v_86 = true
								equipTool(seaManager:RandomTool(), v_86)
							end

							return true
						end
					end
				elseif str9 == "Ghoul" then
					if workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies") and localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
								if child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (localPlayer2.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 500 then
									attack(child)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end, sea == 3)

		CheckRelicChuaDat = function(arg2)
			for _, descendant in pairs(arg2:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and descendant.Enabled then
					return true
				end
			end
		end

		GetRelicChuaDat = function(arg2)
			for k, v_85 in next, arg2, nil do
				if string.find(k, "RelicModel") and CheckRelicChuaDat(v_85) then
					return v_85, k
				end
			end
		end

		GetRelicChuanbiDat = function(arg2)
			for k, v_85 in next, arg2, nil do
				if string.find(k, "RelicModel") and v_85.PrimaryPart:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition") and CheckRelicChuaDat(v_85) then
					return v_85, k
				end
			end
		end

		CheckModelTrialDraco = function()
			local tbl38 = {}
			v28 = workspace:WaitForChild("Map"):WaitForChild("DracoTrial")

			for _, v_85 in pairs({
				"Relic1",
				"Relic2",
				"Relic3",
				"EndRelic1",
				"EndRelic2",
				"EndRelic3",
				"Door1",
				"Door2",
				"Door3",
				"Brazier1",
				"Brazier2",
				"Brazier3",
				"Center",
				"EndPlatform",
				"TeleportOut",
			}) do
				tbl38[v_85] = v28:FindFirstChild(v_85, true)
			end

			local v_85 = next
			local children, v_86 = workspace._WorldOrigin:GetChildren()

			for _, v_87 in v_85, children, v_86 do
				if v_87:IsA("Model") and v_87.Name == "Relic" then
					local meshPart = v_87:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("MeshPart")

					if meshPart.Color == Color3.fromRGB(132, 203, 0) then
						tbl38.RelicModel1 = v_87
					end

					if meshPart.Color == Color3.fromRGB(232, 106, 110) then
						tbl38.RelicModel2 = v_87
					end

					if meshPart.Color == Color3.fromRGB(191, 153, 0) then
						tbl38.RelicModel3 = v_87
					end
				end
			end

			return tbl38
		end

		fn34("TrialDraco", function()

					local v_85 = CheckModelTrialDraco()

					local v_88, v_89 = GetRelicChuanbiDat(v_85)

					if v_88 then
						local v_90 = v_85["EndRelic" .. v_89:split("RelicModel")[2]]
						local proximityPrompt = v_90:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)

							new(v_90.WorldPivot)
						else
							wait(2)
							fireproximityprompt(proximityPrompt)
							wait(2)
						end
					elseif v_86 then
						local v_90 = v_85["Relic" .. v_87:split("RelicModel")[2]]

							new(v_90.WorldPivot)
						else
							wait(2)
							fireproximityprompt(proximityPrompt)
							wait(2)
						end
					end

					return "Doing Draco Trial"
				end

				game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.DracoTrial:InvokeServer()
				wait(3)
			end
		end, sea == 3)

										game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "Continue")

		CanChoosePoint = function()
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				if game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check") and game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check").HadPoint then
					return true
				end
			end)

			absd = ok
			bbbs = result

			if absd and bbbs then
				return true
			end
			return

		end

		InstantChooseGear = function()
			v14 = game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check")

			if v14 and v14.HadPoint then
				condimemeaymeci = v14.RaceDetails
				lastCF = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
				game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].Prompt.CFrame

				if (workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].Prompt.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 3 then
					task.wait()
					fireproximityprompt(workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].Prompt.ProximityPrompt)
					task.wait(0.5)

					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Map["Temple of Time"].InnerClock:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("Highlight") and child.Highlight.Enabled then
							spawn(function()
								fireremotechoosegear(child.Name)
							end)
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.5)

					repeat
						GetOutTempleGui()
						task.wait()
					until not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.TempleGui.Enabled

					game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = lastCF
				end

				task.wait(5)
			end
		end

		DetectGearUp = function(arg2)
			local Buttons = require(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.TempleGui.LocalScriptTemple.Buttons)
			arg2 = arg2 or game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check")
			if type(arg2) ~= "table" then
				return
			end
			local flag14 = arg2.HadPoint == true
			local canSelect = arg2.RaceLevel >= 2
			Buttons.Gear1.GearType = "Default"
			Buttons.Gear4.GearType = "Default"
			Buttons.Gear5.GearType = "Default"
			Buttons.Gear2.GearType = "Alpha"
			Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
			Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
			Buttons.Gear2.GearType = arg2.RaceDetails.Gears[1] == "A" and "Alpha" or arg2.RaceDetails.Gears[1] == "B" and "Omega" or "Blank"
			Buttons.Gear3.GearType = arg2.RaceDetails.Gears[2] == "A" and "Alpha" or arg2.RaceDetails.Gears[2] == "B" and "Omega" or "Blank"
			Buttons.Gear4.GearType = arg2.RaceDetails.Gears[3] == "A" and "Alpha" or arg2.RaceDetails.Gears[3] == "B" and "Omega" or "Blank"
			local gear2 = Buttons.Gear2
			local unlocked2

			if arg2.RaceDetails.A + arg2.RaceDetails.B >= 0 then
				unlocked2 = canSelect
			else
				unlocked2 = false
			end

			gear2.Unlocked = unlocked2
			local gear3 = Buttons.Gear3
			local unlocked3

			if 1 <= arg2.RaceDetails.A + arg2.RaceDetails.B then
				unlocked3 = canSelect
			else
				unlocked3 = false
			end

			gear3.Unlocked = unlocked3
			local gear4 = Buttons.Gear4
			local unlocked4

			if arg2.RaceDetails.A + arg2.RaceDetails.B >= 2 then
				unlocked4 = canSelect
			else
				unlocked4 = false
			end

			gear4.Unlocked = unlocked4
			Buttons.Gear5.CanSelect = false
			Buttons.Gear5.Unlocked = false

			if arg2.RaceDetails.C >= 1 then
				Buttons.Gear5.Unlocked = true
			end

			Buttons.Gear1.Unlocked = true

			if not canSelect then
				Buttons.Gear1.CanSelect = true
				Buttons.Gear1.GearType = "Blank"
				flag14 = true
			else
				Buttons.Gear1.CanSelect = false
				Buttons.Gear1.GearType = "Default"
			end

			if not flag14 then
				Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
				Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
				Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
			else
				local gear22 = Buttons.Gear2
				local v_87

				if arg2.RaceDetails.A + arg2.RaceDetails.B == 0 then
					v_87 = canSelect
				else
					v_87 = false
				end

				gear22.CanSelect = v_87
				local gear32 = Buttons.Gear3
				local canSelect2

				if arg2.RaceDetails.A + arg2.RaceDetails.B == 1 then
					canSelect2 = canSelect
				else
					canSelect2 = false
				end

				gear32.CanSelect = canSelect2
				local gear42 = Buttons.Gear4

				if not (arg2.RaceDetails.A + arg2.RaceDetails.B >= 2) then
					canSelect = false
				end

				gear42.CanSelect = canSelect

				if 3 <= arg2.RaceDetails.A + arg2.RaceDetails.B then
					Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = true
					Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = true
					Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = true

					if Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Omega" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Alpha" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Omega" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
						Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Omega" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
						Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Alpha" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
						Buttons.Gear2.CanSelect = false
					elseif Buttons.Gear2.GearType == "Alpha" and Buttons.Gear3.GearType == "Omega" and Buttons.Gear4.GearType == "Alpha" then
						Buttons.Gear4.CanSelect = false
						Buttons.Gear3.CanSelect = false
					end
				end
			end

			for i = 1, 5 do
				local v_87 = Buttons["Gear" .. i]
				if v_87 and v_87.CanSelect then
					return "Gear" .. i
				end
			end
		end

		ChooseGearV4 = function()
			local commF = game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_
			local response3 = commF:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check")
			if not response3 or not response3.HadPoint then
				return
			end
			local v_87 = DetectGearUp(response3)
			if not v_87 then
				return
			end
			commF:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "SpendPoint", v_87, "Alpha")
			local response4 = commF:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "Check")

			if response4 and response4.HadPoint and DetectGearUp(response4) == v_87 then
				commF:InvokeServer("TempleClock", "SpendPoint", v_87, "Omega")
			end
		end

		end)

		local function fn45()
			if level.Value < 2300 or unlocked["Skull Guitar"] then
				return nil
			end
			local v_87 = count["Bones"]
			local ectoplasm2 = count.Ectoplasm
			local darkFragment = count["Dark Fragment"]

			if v_87 >= 500 and ectoplasm2 >= 250 and darkFragment ~= 0 then
				local progress = lib2:GetProgress("SkullGuitar", "GuitarPuzzleProgress", "Check")

				if progress then
					local gravestones = progress.Gravestones
					local trophies = progress.Trophies
					local swamp = progress.Swamp
					local ghost = progress.Ghost

					if progress.Pipes then
						local v_88 = 5

						if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(tbl34.BuySkullGuitar.Position) <= v_88 then
							fireRemote("soulGuitarBuy")
						else
							new(tbl34.BuySkullGuitar)
						end

						return true
					end

					if trophies then
						fireRemote("GuitarPuzzleProgress", "Pipes")
					elseif ghost then
						fireRemote("GuitarPuzzleProgress", "Trophies")
					elseif gravestones then
						fireRemote("GuitarPuzzleProgress", "Ghost")
					elseif swamp then
						fireRemote("GuitarPuzzleProgress", "Gravestones")
					else
						local livingZombie = enemySpawned("Living Zombie")

						if livingZombie and livingZombie.PrimaryPart then
							attack(livingZombie)
						else
							new(tbl34.Zombies)
						end
					end

					return true
				end

				if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(tbl34.GravestoneEvent.Position) <= 5 then
					fireRemote("gravestoneEvent", 2, true)
				else
					new(tbl34.GravestoneEvent)
				end

				return true
			end

			if sea == 3 then
				if darkFragment == 0 then
					lib2:TravelTo(2)
				elseif ectoplasm2 < 250 then
					lib2:TravelTo(2)
				end
			elseif sea == 2 then
				if ectoplasm2 >= 250 and darkFragment ~= 0 then
					lib2:TravelTo(3)
				end
			end

			if ectoplasm2 <= 250 then
				if sea == 2 then
					return handlers2.Ectoplasm()
				end
			elseif darkFragment == 0 then
				if sea == 2 then
					if isSpawned("Darkbeard") then
						return handlers2.Darkbeard()
					end

					if not VerifyTool("Fist of Darkness") then
						return handlers2[_G.SelectSkullMethod]()
					end
					return handlers2.Darkbeard()
				end
			elseif v_87 <= 500 then
				if sea == 3 then
					return handlers2.Bones()
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn46()
			if game.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") then
				if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("DracoTrial") then
					local lGliseDeProphTie = locations:FindFirstChild("l'Église de Prophétie")
					local island5 = findNearbyLocation("Island 5")
					local island4 = findNearbyLocation("Island 4")

					if lGliseDeProphTie and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(lGliseDeProphTie.Position) <= 150 then
						local Awakener = fireRemote("Awakener", "Check")

						if type(Awakener) ~= "table" then
							if Awakener ~= 0 then
								return true, fireRemote("Awakener", "Teleport")
							end
						else
							if fragments.Value < (Awakener.Cost or 0) then
								return true, fireRemote("Awakener", "Teleport")
							end
							fireRemote("Awakener", "Awaken")
							fireRemote("Awakener", "Teleport")
						end
					end

					if raidManager:IsRaiding() then
						local raidIsland = lib2:GetRaidIsland()

						if raidIsland then
							if not island5 and not island4 then
								local v_87, v_88, v_89 = ipairs(enemies:GetChildren())
								local exitTo = nil
								local v_90

								while true do
									local v_91
									v_91, v_90 = v_87(v_88, v_89)

									if v_91 == nil then
										exitTo = 1
										break
									else
										local primaryPart = v_90.PrimaryPart
										if not (isAlive(v_90) and primaryPart and (raidIsland.Position - primaryPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1000 and primaryPart.Position.Y > 0) then
											v_89 = v_91
											continue
										end
										break
									end
								end

								if exitTo ~= 1 then
									return true, attack(v_90, true, true)
								end
							end

							if island5 or island4 then
								lib2.KillAura()
							end

							if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(raidIsland.Position) <= 3000 then
								new(raidIsland.CFrame + vector2)
							end

							return true
						end

						return true

					elseif VerifyTool("Special Microchip") then
						return true, raidManager:start()
					end
				end
			end
		end

			local function fn47()

			IsAlive = function(arg2)
				return arg2 and arg2.Parent and not arg2:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") and arg2:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and arg2.Humanoid.Health > 0 and arg2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			end

			Convert_CFrame = function(arg2)
				if not arg2 then
					return
				end
				return typeof(arg2) == "Vector3" and CFrame.new(arg2) or typeof(arg2) == "CFrame" and arg2 or typeof(arg2) == "Model" and arg2:GetPivot() or arg2.CFrame
			end

			GetDistance = function(arg2, arg3, arg4)
				if arg2 == nil then
					return
				end

				if not game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					return 9e9
				end

				if arg3 == nil then
					arg3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
				end

				return (Vector3.new(Convert_CFrame(arg2).X, arg4 and 0 or Convert_CFrame(arg2).Y, Convert_CFrame(arg2).Z) - Vector3.new(Convert_CFrame(arg3).X, arg4 and 0 or Convert_CFrame(arg3).Y, Convert_CFrame(arg3).Z)).Magnitude

			end

							local v_93 = true
							if v_92:GetAttribute("KitsuneMarkActive") == v_93 then
								return v_92
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

				local v_87 = next

				for _, v_90 in v_87, children, v_88 do
					if v_90 and v_90.Name ~= "Blank Buddy" and v_90.Name ~= "Player" and v_90.Parent and IsAlive(v_90) and GetDistance(v_90.PrimaryPart.Position) <= 500 and GetDistance(v_90.PrimaryPart.Position) <= huge then
						huge = GetDistance(v_90.PrimaryPart.Position)
						v_89 = v_90
					end
				end

				return v_89
			end

		local function fn47(arg2)
			if not arg2 then
				return 0
			end
			local v_87 = 0

			for _, descendant in pairs(arg2:GetDescendants()) do
				if (descendant:IsA("Seat") or descendant:IsA("VehicleSeat")) and descendant.Occupant then
					local playerFromCharacter = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(descendant.Occupant.Parent)

					if playerFromCharacter then
						for _, v_88 in pairs(_G.SelectPlayerBoatAccount) do
							if playerFromCharacter.Name == v_88 then
								v_87 += 1
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return v_87
		end

			end

			return false
		end

		fn34("FindFullyFrozen", function()
			local leviathanGate = map:FindFirstChild("LeviathanGate")

							descendant.CanCollide = false

					end

					return "Waiting Frozen Dimension Spawn"
				end

				local v_87 = CheckOwnerBoat()

				if fn47(v_87) == 6 then
					if seaManager:GetPlayerBoat() then
						seaManager:RandomTeleport("inf")
						return "Finding Frozen Dimension"
					end
					seaManager:BuyBeast()
					return "Buying Boat"
				end
			end

			if leviathanGate then
				if game.Workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Frozen Dimension") then
					new(workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Frozen Dimension").CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 500, 0))
					return true
				end
			end
		end, sea == 3)

		fn34("FullyLeviathan", function()
			local response3 = ReplicatedStorage2.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("InfoLeviathan", "1")

			if response3 ~= 5 then

			ConvertTo = function(arg2, arg3)
				return arg2.new(arg3.X, arg3.Y, arg3.Z)

			CaculateDistance = function(arg2, arg3)
				if not arg2 then
					return 0
				end
				local cFrame = arg3 or game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
				return (ConvertTo(Vector3, arg2) - ConvertTo(Vector3, cFrame)).magnitude
			end

				local huge = math.huge
				local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
				character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not character then
					return nil
				end
				local v_87 = nil

							local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - character.Position).Magnitude

							if magnitude < huge then
								huge = magnitude
								v_87 = child
							end
						end
					end
				end

					if child:IsA("Model") then

						local v_88 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if magnitude < huge then
								huge = magnitude
								v_87 = child
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return v_87
			end

		fn34("PrehistoricIsland", function()
			local prehistoricIsland = map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")

			if prehistoricIsland then
				if prehistoricIsland:GetAttribute("IsMinigameActive") then
					local now2 = tick()
					RemoveCanTouch = tick()
					n31 = now2

					if DetectLava() then
						local v_87 = next
						local descendants, v_88 = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()

						for _, v_89 in v_87, descendants, v_88 do
							if v_89.Name == "TouchInterest" and v_89.Parent.Name ~= "TrialTeleport" then
								v_89:Destroy()
							end
						end
					end

					if 0 < #workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.InteriorLava:GetChildren() then
						DeleteLava()
					end

					for _, child in pairs(game.Workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.VolcanoRocks:GetChildren()) do
						local vfxLayer = child:FindFirstChild("VFXLayer")

						if vfxLayer then
							local at0 = vfxLayer:FindFirstChild("At0")

							if at0 then
								local glow = at0:FindFirstChild("Glow")

								if glow and glow.Enabled then
									new(vfxLayer.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 0))

									if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(vfxLayer.Position) <= 17 then
										if _G.SkullPre then
											if unlocked["Skull Guitar"] then
												if VerifyTool("Skull Guitar") then
													lib2.FastAttack:ShootInTarget(vfxLayer.Position + vector3)
													equipTool("Skull Guitar")
												else
													fireRemote("LoadItem", "Skull Guitar")
												end
											else
												local v_87 = seaManager:RandomTool()

												if v_87 then
													equipTool(v_87, true)
												end

												UseSkills(vfxLayer, _G.SelectSkill)
											end
										else
											local v_87 = seaManager:RandomTool()

											if v_87 then
												equipTool(v_87, true)
											end

											UseSkills(vfxLayer, _G.SelectSkill)
										end
									end

									return "Volcano Patch"
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local trialTeleport = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("TrialTeleport")

				if trialTeleport and not trialTeleport:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
					local activationPrompt = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Core") and workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild("ActivationPrompt")
					if not activationPrompt then
						return "Waiting..."
					end

					if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(activationPrompt.Position) > 3 then
						n31 = tick()
						new(activationPrompt.CFrame)
						return "Teleporting to Prehistoric Island"
					end

					if activationPrompt:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") and activationPrompt.ProximityPrompt.Enabled then
						n31 = tick()
						fireproximityprompt(activationPrompt.ProximityPrompt)
						task.wait(0.5)
						return "Waiting..."
					end
				end

				if _G.ResetPH then
					local v_87 = 25
					if tick() - n31 >= v_87 and isAlive(localPlayer.Character) and trialTeleport and trialTeleport:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
						prehistoricIsland:Destroy()
						return "Reseting..."
					end
				end
			end

			if not prehistoricIsland then
				local playerBoat = seaManager:GetPlayerBoat()
				if not playerBoat then
					seaManager:BuyNewBoat()
					return true
				end

				if rzSettings.DestroyBoat then
					DeleteBoat()
				end

				local sea2 = rzEnabledOptions.Sea or rzEnabledOptions.KitsuneIsland and map:FindFirstChild("KitsuneIsland")
				local leviathanGate

				if sea2 then
					leviathanGate = sea2
				else
					leviathanGate = rzEnabledOptions.FindFrozen and map:FindFirstChild("LeviathanGate")
				end

				if leviathanGate or rzEnabledOptions.FindMirage and map:FindFirstChild("MysticIsland") then
					return nil
				end

				if playerBoat then
					seaManager:RandomTeleport("inf")
					return true
				end
			end
		end, sea == 3)

						if true then
							local v_95, v_96 = v_92(v_93, v_94)

							if v_95 == nil then
								exitTo = 2
								break
							elseif v_96 then
								boat = seaManager:GetBoat(v_95)
								if not boat then
									v_94 = v_95
									continue
								end
							else
								v_94 = v_95
								continue
							end

							break
						else
							exitTo = 1
							break
						end
					end

					if exitTo ~= 1 then
						if exitTo ~= 2 then
							seaManager:attackSeaEvent(boat)
							return true
						end
					end

					if seaManager:RepairBoat(v1127) then
						return true
					end

					if rzSettings.DestroyBoat then
						DeleteBoat()
					end

					if playerBoat then
						seaManager:RandomTeleport()
						return true
					end
					return
				else
					if v_91 and v_90 ~= "Sea Beast" then
						seaEvent = seaManager:GetSeaEvent(v_90)
						if seaEvent then
							break
						end
						v_89 = v_90
						continue
					end

					v_89 = v_90
				end
			end

			seaManager:attackSeaEvent(seaEvent)
			return true
		end, sea == 3)

		fn34("SwordMastery", function()
			for _, v_87 in pairs(getInventoryItems()) do
				if type(v_87) ~= "table" then
					continue
				end

				if v_87.Type ~= "Sword" then
					continue
				end
				local v_88 = 1

				if tonumber(v_87.Mastery) >= v_88 and tonumber(v_87.Mastery) < 600 then
					local name = v_87.Name

					if tonumber(v_87.Mastery) >= 1 and tonumber(v_87.Mastery) < 600 then
						if VerifyTool(name) then
							rzSettings.FarmTool = "Sword"
							if sea == 1 then
								return handlers2.Level()
							end
							return handlers2[_G.SelectMethod]()
						end

						fireRemote("LoadItem", name)
					end

					if v_87.Mastery > 599 then
						if not VerifyTool(name) then
							fireRemote("LoadItem", name)
						end
					end

					return
				end
			end
		end)

		fn34("Level", function()
			local quest = questManager:GetQuest()
			if not quest then
				return nil
			end
			local position = quest.Enemy.Position
			local waitingFor = questManager:VerifyQuest(quest.Enemy.Name)
			if not waitingFor then
				return questManager:StartQuest(quest.Name, quest.Count, questManager:GetQuestPosition(quest.Name))
			end
			local v_87 = enemySpawned(waitingFor)
			if v_87 and v_87.PrimaryPart and not v_87:GetAttribute("EscapeKey") then
				return "Killing: " .. v_87.Name, attack(v_87, true)
			end
			local questPosition = questManager:GetQuestPosition(quest.Name)

			if #position <= 0 then
				if questPosition then
					new(questPosition * questManager._Position)
				end
			else
				playerTeleport:NPCs(position)
			end

			return "Waiting for: " .. waitingFor
		end)

		fn34("Nearest", function()
			local primaryPart = v_84 and v_84.PrimaryPart
			if primaryPart and isAlive(v_84) and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(primaryPart.Position) < 1500 then
				return "Killing: " .. v_84.Name, attack(v_84, true, true)
			end
			local v_87, v_88, v_89 = ipairs(enemies:GetChildren())
			local v_90 = 1500
			local v_91 = nil

			while true do
				local v_92, v_93 = v_87(v_88, v_89)

				if v_92 == nil then
					break
				else
					local primaryPart2 = v_93.PrimaryPart

					if isAlive(v_93) and primaryPart2 then
						local v_94 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(primaryPart2.Position)

						if v_94 < v_90 then
							v_89 = v_92
							v_90 = v_94
							v_91 = v_93
						else
							v_89 = v_92
						end
					else
						v_89 = v_92
					end
				end
			end

			if v_91 then
				v_84 = v_91
				attack(v_91, true, true)
				return true
			end

			task.wait(0.4)
		end)

do
	local function fn34(arg, ...)
		local now2 = tick()
		handlers[arg](handlers, ...)
		print(arg, tick() - now2)
	end

	fn34("Initialize")
	fn34("StartFarm")
	fn34("StartFunctions")

	local function fn35(...)
		local v_82 = table.pack(...)

		if not pcall(function(...)
			task.spawn(fn34, ...)
		end, ...) then
			fn34(table.unpack(v_82, 1, v_82.n))
		end
	end

	fn35("LoadLibrary")
end
